```
User requirement (2026-10-15, after WP-07): when the diagnosis hits a limit, it must return whatever findings it reached so far, never drop the gathered context or fail the run.

**Why:** a capped or failed run that throws away its evidence wastes neurons and leaves the user with nothing; partial results are still useful.

**How to apply:** follow TDD §8.2 "Limits keep progress" in WP-08 (`narrate` keeps well-formed partial streamed text via `LlmUnavailable.partial`) and WP-09 (evidence stored per step, `llmTools({finishOnly: true})` on the last turn, `salvageFindings` + `classify` on any limit, `runs.status` names the limit, one test per limit). Apply the same idea to later flows (watch, postmortem). Related: [[tasks-board-updates]].
```

```
### 7.5 Demo service

| Endpoint | Behavior by mode |
|---|---|
| Public `GET /w/<ws>/` | Returns HTML containing `<link rel=stylesheet href="/w/<ws>/static/app.css">` and `<script src="/w/<ws>/static/app.js">`. Also `GET /w/<ws>/static/*`. |
| `healthy` | 200. Pages use `cache-control: public, max-age=0, must-revalidate`. Static files use `public, max-age=31536000, immutable`. No cookies. |
| `slow` | Same as healthy, but every response waits 3 s first |
| `errors` | 500 for all paths |
| `redirect_loop` | `/w/<ws>/` sends a 302 to `/w/<ws>/r`, which sends a 302 back to `/w/<ws>/` |
| `bad_cache` | Static files get `cache-control: no-store`, and every response gets `Set-Cookie: demo_session=…` |

`DemoAdmin extends WorkerEntrypoint` has `getMode(ws)` and `setMode(ws, mode)`. Both use `DemoState` (`idFromName(ws)`) and validate the mode with zod. There's no public admin route.

### 7.6 Action catalog (`actions/catalog.ts`)

| ID | Args | Proposable when | Execute | Verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `restore_demo_service` | `{}` | Target host = demo host, path starts with `/w/<incident.workspaceId>/`, category ≠ `healthy` | `env.DEMO.setMode(ws, "healthy")` | `quickCheck` pass, then `afterAction` |
| `add_to_watchlist` | `{intervalSec: 300}` | Not already watched and under caps | Insert into `watches` | None; outcome goes on the timeline |
| `recheck_burst` | `{}` | No monitor active | Agent `monitor(kind:"burst")` | Ticks |
| `generate_postmortem` | `{}` | status = `resolved` | `generatePostmortem()` | None |

The Workflow drops any action that isn't proposable. At most one action per run.

## 8. Core flows

### 8.1 New incident (chat)

1. `POST /api/incidents {message}`. The Worker parses the target (D9), checks the run quota (D14, from WP-18), creates the ID, and calls `agent.init(...)`.
2. `init` writes the incident, message and timeline `opened` rows, upserts the index, and calls `startRun(message, "initial")`.

### 16:24 · WP-10 Chat UI

> Lets plan WP-10 now.

(Plan approved: routes `/` and `/i/:id`; `useIncident` over `useAgent` with a stream buffer; check cards with lazy evidence; a small safe markdown renderer with clickable `[http-1]` chips; an other-host follow-up offer; a read-only view for link visitors (new `whoami` callable); limit and rule-based notes. Pure helpers are unit-tested; the UI is checked with headless-browser screenshots in light and dark.)

(WP-10 done in `dea9b32`; 443 tests green, build OK. A headless browser walkthrough with the mock LLM covered: create → live checks → streamed narration → evidence → chip scroll → follow-up → other-host offer → read-only visitor, in light, dark and at phone width. The screenshots caught narrow-screen layout, a sticky header lost on scroll, a hidden view-only banner and a first-load Vite reload, all fixed.)
```

```
// The integration project's only "internet" (outboundService): DoH answers for *.example.com and a few
// test sites, so Workflow runs are offline and deterministic. nxdomain.example.com doesn't exist,
// broken.example.com returns 500, every other *.example.com host serves a small HTML page.
const fakeInternet = `
const dns = (body) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { headers: { "content-type": "application/dns-json" } });
export default {
  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    if (url.hostname === "cloudflare-dns.com" || url.hostname === "dns.google") {
      const name = (url.searchParams.get("name") || "").replace(/\\.$/, "");
      if (!name.endsWith("example.com") || name === "nxdomain.example.com") return dns({ Status: 3 });
      const a = url.searchParams.get("type") === "A";
      return dns({ Status: 0, Answer: a ? [{ name, type: 1, TTL: 300, data: "93.184.215.14" }] : [] });
    }
    if (url.hostname === "broken.example.com") return new Response("upstream exploded", { status: 500 });
    if (url.hostname.endsWith("example.com")) return new Response("<html><body>ok</body></html>", { headers: { "content-type": "text/html" } });
    return new Response("fake internet: unknown host " + url.hostname, { status: 502 });
  },
};
`;
```

```
TDD.md:582:  - **Dev-only exception:** when `SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN` is set, that one exact origin (scheme, host and port, for example the local demo service) skips the checks above. It's set only in `.dev.vars` for local work and never in `wrangler.jsonc` `vars`, so in production the demo is reached through its public `workers.dev` URL and everything private stays blocked. There's no wildcard and no partial match.
```

````
## 7. Interfaces

### 7.1 HTTP API (Hono, all paths need the workspace cookie, which is created on first request)

| Method and path | Body / query | Response | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/session` | | `{demoUrl, budgetMode, limits}` | Sets `__Host-ws` (HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite=Lax, 1 year) if it's missing |
| `GET /api/incidents` | | `[{id,target,status,origin,unread,updatedAt}]` | Sidebar polling (D16) |
| `POST /api/incidents` | `{message}` | `{id}` / 400 `{error:"need_target"}` / 429 `{retryAfter}` / 503 `{error:"demo_unavailable"}` | Target parse (D9), then quota (D14), then `IncidentAgent.init`. 503 while `DEMO_BASE_URL` still holds the `<sub>` placeholder |
| `GET /api/incidents/:id` | | Snapshot (`IncidentState` + findings) | Owner only. Used by eval and smoke tests. |
| `POST /api/incidents/:id/read` | | 204 | Clears `unread` |
| `GET /api/demo` · `POST /api/demo/mode` | `{mode}` | `{mode, url}` | Calls `env.DEMO.setMode(ws, mode)` |
| `GET/POST /api/watches` · `DELETE /api/watches/:id` | `{url, intervalSec}` | Watch list | Per-workspace and global caps |
| `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/webhook` | `{url}` | `{kind, hint}` | Allowlist, then encryption |
| `POST /api/voice` | `audio/webm` body | `{text}` | Size cap, then limit, then whisper, then normalization |
| `GET /api/health` | | `{ok, version}` | Smoke test |

### 7.2 IncidentAgent: RPC (Worker and Workflow) and callables (client)

```ts
// RPC (server-side only)
init(p: { workspaceId; target; origin: "chat"|"watch"; message?: string; watchId?: string }): Promise<void>
getRunContext(runId): Promise<RunContext>          // prior evidence digests, convo tail, budget mode
addPendingChecks(runId, calls: PlannedCall[]): Promise<string[]>   // allocates evidence IDs
recordCheck(evidenceId, result: ToolResult): Promise<void>         // upsert → idempotent
setFindings(runId, f: Findings): Promise<void>
narrate(runId): Promise<{ messageId: string }>                    // streams; idempotent per runId (D4)
proposeAction(runId, action: ProposedAction, workflowId): Promise<{ approvalId: string | null }>
recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId, outcome): Promise<void>
afterAction(runId, verify: QuickCheckResult): Promise<void>        // → mitigated + monitor schedule
reportWatchCheck(pass: boolean, digest): Promise<void>             // from cron
// @callable (WebSocket). Every method except getEvidence requires role=owner (D8)
sendMessage(text) · approve(approvalId) · reject(approvalId) · resolve() · generatePostmortem()
markRead() · getEvidence(evidenceId)
```

`onConnect(conn, ctx)` parses the cookie from `ctx.request` and calls `conn.setState({ role })`. Owner-only callables use `getCurrentAgent().connection.state.role`.

*WP-08 as built:*
- **Signatures:** `addPendingChecks(runId, turn, calls)`; `setFindings(runId, findings, mode)`; `finishRun(runId, status, {toolCalls, llmTurns})` (first call wins); `startRun(question, kind)` throws `run_active`; plus `getSnapshot()`.
- **Viewers:** `shouldConnectionBeReadonly` also marks them readonly. A socket for an incident that doesn't exist is closed with 4404.
- **Routing:** the Worker only routes WebSocket upgrades to `/agents/incident-agent/<32-hex id>`. Every other agent path and plain HTTP gets a 404, so no stray Durable Objects are created.
- **Callables without decorator syntax:** `@callable()` only records the method in the SDK's registry (the decorator context is unused), so `IncidentAgent.ts` registers `sendMessage`, `resolve`, `markRead` and `getEvidence` with `callable()(fn)` after the class. That avoids the Babel decorator transform and the ES2021 target the SDK otherwise needs in Vite and Vitest.
- **Typed stub:** `agent/stub.ts` `incidentAgent(env, id)` wraps `getAgentByName`, whose type only accepts agents with `unknown` state.
- **Deferred to their WPs:** the Workflow launch (WP-09, marked in `startRun`); `proposeAction`, `recordApprovalOutcome`, `afterAction`, approve/reject and the monitor (WP-12); quotas (WP-17); `reportWatchCheck` (WP-18); `generatePostmortem` (WP-22).
````

```
import { Hono } from "hono";
import type { AppEnv } from "../env";
import { LIMITS } from "../lib/limits";

export function demoUrlFor(baseUrl: string, workspaceId: string): string {
  return `${baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "")}/w/${workspaceId}/`;
}

export const session = new Hono<AppEnv>().get("/", (c) =>
  c.json({
    demoUrl: demoUrlFor(c.env.DEMO_BASE_URL, c.get("workspaceId")),
    // WP-17 wires this to the neuron budget guard (TDD §11).
    budgetMode: "ai" as "ai" | "rule_based",
    limits: LIMITS,
  }),
);
import { Hono } from "hono";
import { incidentAgent } from "../agent/stub";
import type { AppEnv } from "../env";
import { isId, newId } from "../lib/ids";
import { parseTarget, type TargetResult } from "../safety/target";
import { demoUrlFor } from "./session";

type IndexRow = { id: string; target: string; status: string; origin: string; unread: number; updated_at: number };

const MAX_MESSAGE = 2000;

/** Incident API (TDD §7.1). Every route is scoped to the caller's workspace cookie. */
export const incidents = new Hono<AppEnv>()
  .post("/", async (c) => {
    const body = await c.req.json<{ message?: unknown }>().catch(() => ({}) as { message?: unknown });
    const message = typeof body.message === "string" ? body.message.trim().slice(0, MAX_MESSAGE) : "";
    if (!message) return c.json({ error: "empty_message" }, 400);
    const workspaceId = c.get("workspaceId");
    let target: TargetResult;
    try {
      target = parseTarget(message, demoUrlFor(c.env.DEMO_BASE_URL, workspaceId));
    } catch {
      // DEMO_BASE_URL still holds the `<sub>` placeholder (set in WP-11 locally, WP-13 deployed).
      return c.json({ error: "demo_unavailable" }, 503);
    }
    if (!target.ok) return c.json({ error: target.error }, 400);
    // WP-17: run quota (D14) before init.
    const id = newId();
    const agent = await incidentAgent(c.env, id);
    await agent.init({ workspaceId, target: target.url, origin: "chat", message });
    return c.json({ id });
  })
  .get("/", async (c) => {
    const { results } = await c.env.DB.prepare(
      "SELECT id, target, status, origin, unread, updated_at FROM incidents_index WHERE workspace_id = ? ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 50",
    )
      .bind(c.get("workspaceId"))
      .all<IndexRow>();
    return c.json(results.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, target: r.target, status: r.status, origin: r.origin, unread: r.unread === 1, updatedAt: r.updated_at })));
  })
  .get("/:id", async (c) => {
    const id = c.req.param("id");
    if (!(await owns(c.env.DB, c.get("workspaceId"), id))) return c.json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
    const agent = await incidentAgent(c.env, id);
    return c.json(await agent.getSnapshot());
  })
  .post("/:id/read", async (c) => {
    const id = c.req.param("id");
    if (!(await owns(c.env.DB, c.get("workspaceId"), id))) return c.json({ error: "not_found" }, 404);
    await (await incidentAgent(c.env, id)).markRead();
    return c.body(null, 204);
  });

async function owns(db: D1Database, workspaceId: string, id: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!isId(id)) return false;
  return !!(await db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM incidents_index WHERE id = ? AND workspace_id = ?").bind(id, workspaceId).first());
}
import { dohQuery, RESOLVERS, RR_TYPES, type Fetch } from "../lib/doh";
import { isBlockedIp, parseIpv4 } from "./iprange";

/**
 * SSRF guard (TDD §11). Shape checks need no network; `createGuard` adds the DoH check of every
 * A/AAAA answer and caches it per host for the lifetime of one tool call.
 */
export type SsrfCode =
  | "invalid_url"
  | "scheme"
  | "port"
  | "credentials"
  | "ip_literal"
  | "local_name"
  | "private_address"
  | "no_addresses"
  | "dns_error";

export type GuardOk = { ok: true; url: URL; devAllowed?: true };
export type GuardFail = { ok: false; code: SsrfCode; message: string };
export type GuardResult = GuardOk | GuardFail;
export type Guard = (url: string) => Promise<GuardResult>;

const ALLOWED_PORTS = new Set(["", "80", "443"]);

function fail(code: SsrfCode, message: string): GuardFail {
  return { ok: false, code, message };
}

/** Reads the dev-only exception without adding it to the generated `Env` (it only exists in `.dev.vars`). */
export function devAllowOrigin(env: object): string | undefined {
  const value = (env as { SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN?: unknown }).SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN;
  return typeof value === "string" && value !== "" ? value : undefined;
}

function sameOrigin(url: URL, allowed: string | undefined): boolean {
  if (!allowed) return false;
  try {
    return url.origin === new URL(allowed).origin;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function checkUrlShape(raw: string, allowOrigin?: string): GuardResult {
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(raw);
  } catch {
    return fail("invalid_url", "That isn't a valid URL.");
  }
  if (sameOrigin(url, allowOrigin)) return { ok: true, url, devAllowed: true };

  if (url.protocol !== "http:" && url.protocol !== "https:") {
    return fail("scheme", `Only http and https are allowed, not ${url.protocol.replace(":", "")}.`);
  }
  if (!ALLOWED_PORTS.has(url.port)) return fail("port", `Only ports 80 and 443 are allowed, not ${url.port}.`);
  if (url.username || url.password) return fail("credentials", "URLs with credentials aren't allowed.");

  const host = url.hostname.replace(/\.$/, "");
  if (host.startsWith("[") || parseIpv4(host) !== null) {
    const kind = isBlockedIp(host) ? "a private or reserved address" : "an IP address";
    return fail("ip_literal", `${host} is ${kind}. Give a public hostname instead.`);
  }
  if (
    host === "localhost" ||
    host.endsWith(".localhost") ||
    host.endsWith(".local") ||
    host.endsWith(".internal") ||
    !host.includes(".")
  ) {
    return fail("local_name", `${host} is a local or internal name, not a public hostname.`);
  }
  return { ok: true, url };
}

/** Resolves A and AAAA over DoH and refuses the host if any answer is private or reserved. */
import { LinkProvider, type LinkComponentProps } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { forwardRef, useEffect } from "react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { AppShell } from "./components/AppShell";
import { IncidentSidebar } from "./components/IncidentSidebar";
import { getSession } from "./lib/api";
import { AppRoutes } from "./routes";

/** Kumo links (sidebar items, link buttons) navigate inside the app instead of reloading. */
const AppLink = forwardRef<HTMLAnchorElement, LinkComponentProps>(({ href, to, ...rest }, ref) => <Link ref={ref} href={href ?? to ?? "/"} {...rest} />);
AppLink.displayName = "AppLink";

export function App() {
  // Creates the workspace cookie up front, so the first WebSocket already carries it.
  useEffect(() => void getSession(), []);
  return (
    <LinkProvider component={AppLink}>
      <AppShell sidebar={<IncidentSidebar />} aside={<h2 className="text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">Demo service</h2>}>
        <AppRoutes />
      </AppShell>
    </LinkProvider>
  );
}

        Built on Cloudflare
      </footer>
    </div>
  );

import type { IncidentStatus, Origin } from "../../worker/agent/state";

/** Errors the UI explains in words (TDD §7.1). */
export type ApiError = "need_target" | "empty_message" | "demo_unavailable" | "rate_limited" | "network" | "server";

export const ERROR_TEXT: Record<ApiError, string> = {
  need_target: "Which site is broken? Include a URL or a hostname, like cloudflare.com, or say “demo”.",
  empty_message: "Type a message first.",
  demo_unavailable: "The demo service isn't set up yet. Try a real hostname, like cloudflare.com.",
  rate_limited: "Too many requests. Wait a minute and try again.",
  network: "Couldn't reach the server. Check your connection and try again.",
  server: "Something went wrong on our side. Try again.",
};

export type IncidentRow = { id: string; target: string; status: IncidentStatus; origin: Origin; unread: boolean; updatedAt: number };
export type Session = { demoUrl: string; budgetMode: "ai" | "rule_based" };

type Fetch = typeof fetch;

export async function createIncident(message: string, f: Fetch = fetch): Promise<{ ok: true; id: string } | { ok: false; error: ApiError }> {
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await f("/api/incidents", { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ message }) });
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "network" };
  }
  if (res.ok) return { ok: true, id: ((await res.json()) as { id: string }).id };
  if (res.status === 429) return { ok: false, error: "rate_limited" };
  const body = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
  const known: ApiError[] = ["need_target", "empty_message", "demo_unavailable"];
  return { ok: false, error: known.includes(body.error as ApiError) ? (body.error as ApiError) : "server" };
}

export async function listIncidents(f: Fetch = fetch): Promise<IncidentRow[]> {
  try {
    const res = await f("/api/incidents");
    return res.ok ? ((await res.json()) as IncidentRow[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/** Also creates the workspace cookie on a first visit. */
export async function getSession(f: Fetch = fetch): Promise<Session | null> {
  try {
    const res = await f("/api/session");
    return res.ok ? ((await res.json()) as Session) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Tells the sidebar to reload after something changed (creation, a status change). Polling is WP-19. */
export const INCIDENTS_CHANGED = "incidents:changed";
export const notifyIncidentsChanged = () => window.dispatchEvent(new Event(INCIDENTS_CHANGED));
```

```
/** Rule-based classification (TDD §9.3): first match wins. Pure, so the Workflow can call it inside a step. */
export function classify(results: ToolResult[]): Findings {
  const e = pick(results);
  const m = RULES.reduce<Match | null>((found, rule) => found ?? rule(e), null) ?? inconclusive(e);
  return {
    category: m.category,
    confidence: m.confidence,
    evidence: m.ids,
    cause: m.cause,
    next_steps: NEXT_STEPS[m.category],
    actions: m.category === "healthy" ? [] : ["restore_demo_service"],
  };
}

const ID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/;

/** 128-bit random hex ID for incidents, workspaces and approvals (TDD §6.4). */
export function newId(): string {
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

export function isId(value: unknown): value is string {
  return typeof value === "string" && ID_RE.test(value);
}
```

```
/** First same-origin script or stylesheet in the page (D10). */
export function findAsset(html: string, baseUrl: string): string | null {
  const base = new URL(baseUrl);
  const tags = /<script\b[^>]*?\bsrc\s*=\s*["']?([^"'\s>]+)|<link\b(?=[^>]*\brel\s*=\s*["']?stylesheet\b)[^>]*?\bhref\s*=\s*["']?([^"'\s>]+)/gi;
  for (const m of html.matchAll(tags)) {
    const href = m[1] ?? m[2];
    if (!href) continue;
    try {
      const u = new URL(href, base);
      if (u.origin === base.origin) return u.toString();
    } catch {
      // ignore malformed references
    }
  }
  return null;
}

async function probeAsset(ctx: ToolContext, guard: ReturnType<typeof createGuard>, url: string, now: () => number): Promise<AssetProbe> {
  try {
    const checked = await guard(url);
    if (!checked.ok) return { url, error: checked.code };
    const res = await readOnce(ctx.fetch, checked.url.toString(), "GET", { now });
    if (!res.ok) return { url, error: res.error };
    return { url, status: res.status, cacheControl: res.headers.get("cache-control"), setCookie: res.headers.has("set-cookie") };
  } catch (e) {
    if ((e as Error).name === "BudgetExceededError") return { url, error: "budget" };
    throw e;
  }

- **Header:** wordmark "Incident Copilot", `BudgetBanner`, and a GitHub link. Don't use the Cloudflare logo; "Built on Cloudflare" in the footer is enough.
- **Left `Sidebar`:** "New incident" and the incident list (status badge, unread dot, polled every 10 s), followed by `WatchlistPanel` and `WebhookSettings`.
- **Main area:** `ChatPane`.
- **Right column:** `DemoPanel`, which collapses below the `lg` breakpoint.

### 12.2 Components

- **`ChatPane`:** messages, check cards inline in run order, approval cards, and a composer that's disabled while `activeRunId` is set. The empty state shows chips for "Use demo service" and "diagnose cloudflare.com". The composer includes `VoiceButton`.
- **`CheckCard`:** tool icon, label, and a `StatusBadge` with icon **and** text (`Pending`, `Running` with a `Loader`, `✓ Pass`, `✕ Fail`, `Unavailable`). It shows a summary and expands with `Collapsible` to show evidence in `Code`, which is fetched lazily with `getEvidence`. Clicking an `[http-1]` chip scrolls to the card and flashes an orange ring.
- **`ApprovalCard`:** the action title, why it's proposed (cause plus refs), and **Approve** (orange primary) and **Reject** buttons. It shows the resolved state: approved, rejected, expired or superseded. For viewers, the buttons are hidden and `ReadOnlyBanner` is shown.
- **Status chip in the incident header:** `investigating`, `awaiting approval`, `mitigated` or `resolved`, always with text. It's followed by Resolve and Postmortem buttons.
```

```json
  "content": "# WP-11: Demo service + DemoPanel\n\n## Context\nThe reviewer demo (PRD §4.2, §13) depends on a demo site you can break on purpose: switch it to \"500 errors\", ask \"what's wrong with my demo service?\", then (in WP-12) approve the fix. Right now `demo-service/` is a bare skeleton (`DemoAdmin.ping()` and a plain-text page). \"diagnose demo\" returns `503 demo_unavailable` because `DEMO_BASE_URL` still contains `<sub>`. The spec is TDD §7.5 and the WP-11 checklist in TASKS.md. At the end, every mode behaves as §7.5 describes, the demo can be diagnosed locally end to end, and the right column has a working `DemoPanel`.\n\n## The one real design problem: the local demo URL\nWith `@cloudflare/vite-plugin`, an auxiliary Worker gets **no port of its own**. Only the main Worker is served on `localhost:5173`, and the demo service can only be reached through the `DEMO` binding. Running it separately with `wrangler dev` would start a **second copy with its own state**, so `setMode` would change one copy while the probes read the other.\n\n**Approach (recommended):** the main Worker passes `/w/*` through to the demo service, but **only when `DEMO_BASE_URL` points at the main Worker's own origin**. That's true only in local dev.\n- `.dev.vars`: `DEMO_BASE_URL=http://localhost:5173` and `SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN=http://localhost:5173`.\n- In production, `DEMO_BASE_URL` is the demo's own `workers.dev` URL, so the main Worker never forwards anything and `/w/*` on it returns 404. No new flag is needed.\n- `DemoAdmin` gets a `fetch()` that uses the same page handler. It can only be reached through the binding, so this adds no public admin surface.\n- **Spike first (5 min):** check that a probe from inside workerd can fetch `http://localhost:5173/...` while `global_fetch_strictly_public` is on. **Fallback** if it's blocked: in the Workflow's tool `fetch` (`worker/workflows/diagnosis.ts:39`), send requests whose origin equals `SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN` to `env.DEMO.fetch` (dev only). Whichever path is used goes in the TDD.\n\n## Changes\n\n### Demo Worker (`demo-service/src/`, file layout as in TDD §4)\n- **`modes.ts`:** `DEMO_MODES = [\"healthy\",\"slow\",\"errors\",\"redirect_loop\",\"bad_cache\"]`, a zod `DemoMode` schema and labels for the UI. The main Worker and the web client import it (add it to `tsconfig.web.json` `include`).\n- **`state.ts`:** `DemoState` Durable Object (SQLite-backed) storing the mode in `ctx.storage.kv`, with `getMode()` and `setMode(mode)`. The default is `healthy`.\n- **`pages.ts`:** `handle(request, env)`, which implements §7.5:\n  - `/w/<ws>/` returns HTML with `<link rel=stylesheet href=\"/w/<ws>/static/app.css\">` and `<script src=\"/w/<ws>/static/app.js\">`, so the existing `findAsset` (`worker/tools/http.ts:154`) picks it up.\n  - `/w/<ws>/static/app.css|app.js`; any other static path returns 404.\n  - `/w/<ws>/r` sends a 302 to `/w/<ws>/`. In `redirect_loop` mode, the page itself sends a 302 to `/r`, which makes the loop.\n  - **healthy:** pages get `public, max-age=0, must-revalidate`, static files get `public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, and there are no cookies.\n  - **slow:** same as healthy after a 3 s wait.\n  - **errors:** every path returns 500.\n  - **bad_cache:** static files get `no-store`, and every response gets `Set-Cookie: demo_session=<random>; Path=/w/<ws>/`.\n  - `<ws>` must pass `isId` (`worker/lib/ids.ts`), otherwise 404, so no stray Durable Objects are created.\n  - `/` keeps the plain-text info page.\n  - There's no admin route.\n- **`index.ts`:** `DemoAdmin` gets `getMode(ws)` and `setMode(ws, mode)`. Both are checked with zod (`ws` with `isId`, `mode` with `DemoMode`) and use `DEMO_STATE.idFromName(ws)`. It also gets `fetch()` delegating to `handle`, and keeps `ping()`. The default export delegates to `handle`. `DemoState` is exported.\n- **`demo-service/wrangler.jsonc`:** the `DEMO_STATE` Durable Object binding plus a `v1` `new_sqlite_classes: [\"DemoState\"]` migration. Then run `pnpm cf-typegen`.\n\n### Main Worker\n- **`worker/api/demo.ts`** (new), mounted in `router.ts` behind the workspace cookie:\n  - `GET /api/demo` returns `{mode, url}`;\n  - `POST /api/demo/mode {mode}` checks the mode with zod (400 `invalid_mode`), calls `env.DEMO.setMode(ws, mode)` and returns `{mode, url}`;\n  - both return 503 `demo_unavailable` while the `<sub>` placeholder is still there.\n  - A small `demoTarget(env, ws)` helper in `session.ts` returns the URL or `null`. `incidents.ts` reuses it instead of its own try/catch.\n- **`worker/index.ts`:** the dev pass-through for `/w/*` described above. Add `\"/w/*\"` to `run_worker_first` in `wrangler.jsonc`; otherwise the SPA fallback would answer.\n- **`.dev.vars.example`:** replace the wrong `8788` example with `http://localhost:5173` for both variables, with a one-line reason. ## Approved Plan:
```
```
# WP-11: Demo service + DemoPanel

## Context
The reviewer demo (PRD §4.2, §13) depends on a demo site you can break on purpose: switch it to "500 errors", ask "what's wrong with my demo service?", then (in WP-12) approve the fix. Right now `demo-service/` is a bare skeleton (`DemoAdmin.ping()` and a plain-text page). "diagnose demo" returns `503 demo_unavailable` because `DEMO_BASE_URL` still contains `<sub>`. The spec is TDD §7.5 and the WP-11 checklist in TASKS.md. At the end, every mode behaves as §7.5 describes, the demo can be diagnosed locally end to end, and the right column has a working `DemoPanel`.

## The one real design problem: the local demo URL
With `@cloudflare/vite-plugin`, an auxiliary Worker gets **no port of its own**. Only the main Worker is served on `localhost:5173`, and the demo service can only be reached through the `DEMO` binding. Running it separately with `wrangler dev` would start a **second copy with its own state**, so `setMode` would change one copy while the probes read the other.

**Approach (recommended):** the main Worker passes `/w/*` through to the demo service, but **only when `DEMO_BASE_URL` points at the main Worker's own origin**. That's true only in local dev.
- `.dev.vars`: `DEMO_BASE_URL=http://localhost:5173` and `SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN=http://localhost:5173`.
- In production, `DEMO_BASE_URL` is the demo's own `workers.dev` URL, so the main Worker never forwards anything and `/w/*` on it returns 404. No new flag is needed.
- `DemoAdmin` gets a `fetch()` that uses the same page handler. It can only be reached through the binding, so this adds no public admin surface.
- **Spike first (5 min):** check that a probe from inside workerd can fetch `http://localhost:5173/...` while `global_fetch_strictly_public` is on. **Fallback** if it's blocked: in the Workflow's tool `fetch` (`worker/workflows/diagnosis.ts:39`), send requests whose origin equals `SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN` to `env.DEMO.fetch` (dev only). Whichever path is used goes in the TDD.

## Changes

### Demo Worker (`demo-service/src/`, file layout as in TDD §4)
- **`modes.ts`:** `DEMO_MODES = ["healthy","slow","errors","redirect_loop","bad_cache"]`, a zod `DemoMode` schema and labels for the UI. The main Worker and the web client import it (add it to `tsconfig.web.json` `include`).
- **`state.ts`:** `DemoState` Durable Object (SQLite-backed) storing the mode in `ctx.storage.kv`, with `getMode()` and `setMode(mode)`. The default is `healthy`.
- **`pages.ts`:** `handle(request, env)`, which implements §7.5:
  - `/w/<ws>/` returns HTML with `<link rel=stylesheet href="/w/<ws>/static/app.css">` and `<script src="/w/<ws>/static/app.js">`, so the existing `findAsset` (`worker/tools/http.ts:154`) picks it up.
  - `/w/<ws>/static/app.css|app.js`; any other static path returns 404.
  - `/w/<ws>/r` sends a 302 to `/w/<ws>/`. In `redirect_loop` mode, the page itself sends a 302 to `/r`, which makes the loop.
  - **healthy:** pages get `public, max-age=0, must-revalidate`, static files get `public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, and there are no cookies.
  - **slow:** same as healthy after a 3 s wait.
  - **errors:** every path returns 500.
  - **bad_cache:** static files get `no-store`, and every response gets `Set-Cookie: demo_session=<random>; Path=/w/<ws>/`.
  - `<ws>` must pass `isId` (`worker/lib/ids.ts`), otherwise 404, so no stray Durable Objects are created.
  - `/` keeps the plain-text info page.
  - There's no admin route.
- **`index.ts`:** `DemoAdmin` gets `getMode(ws)` and `setMode(ws, mode)`. Both are checked with zod (`ws` with `isId`, `mode` with `DemoMode`) and use `DEMO_STATE.idFromName(ws)`. It also gets `fetch()` delegating to `handle`, and keeps `ping()`. The default export delegates to `handle`. `DemoState` is exported.
- **`demo-service/wrangler.jsonc`:** the `DEMO_STATE` Durable Object binding plus a `v1` `new_sqlite_classes: ["DemoState"]` migration. Then run `pnpm cf-typegen`.

### Main Worker
- **`worker/api/demo.ts`** (new), mounted in `router.ts` behind the workspace cookie:
  - `GET /api/demo` returns `{mode, url}`;
  - `POST /api/demo/mode {mode}` checks the mode with zod (400 `invalid_mode`), calls `env.DEMO.setMode(ws, mode)` and returns `{mode, url}`;
  - both return 503 `demo_unavailable` while the `<sub>` placeholder is still there.
  - A small `demoTarget(env, ws)` helper in `session.ts` returns the URL or `null`. `incidents.ts` reuses it instead of its own try/catch.
- **`worker/index.ts`:** the dev pass-through for `/w/*` described above. Add `"/w/*"` to `run_worker_first` in `wrangler.jsonc`; otherwise the SPA fallback would answer.
- **`.dev.vars.example`:** replace the wrong `8788` example with `http://localhost:5173` for both variables, with a one-line reason. Set the same values in the local, untracked `.dev.vars`.

### UI
- **`web/components/DemoPanel.tsx`:**
  - a Kumo `Radio` group with the five modes in plain words ("Healthy", "Slow (+3 s)", "500 errors", "Redirect loop", "Bad caching");
  - the demo URL in `ClipboardText`, plus an "Open" link (new tab);
  - a short hint: 'Then ask: "What's wrong with my demo service?"';
  - the change is shown straight away and undone with an error message if the save fails;
  - a 503 shows "not set up";
  - it reloads on a `demo:changed` window event, so WP-12's restore can update it.
- **`web/lib/api.ts`:** `getDemo` and `setDemoMode`.
- **`web/App.tsx`:** replace the placeholder `aside` with `<DemoPanel />`.
- **`AppShell.tsx`:** TDD §12.1 says the panel "collapses below `lg`", but today it's simply hidden. Below `lg`, show it as a closed `Collapsible` ("Demo service") at the top of `<main>`. A small media-query hook keeps only one copy mounted.

### Tests
- **`test/demo/demo.test.ts`**, through `exports.default.fetch` and the `DemoAdmin` RPC:
  - each mode's status, headers and cookies, for the page and a static file;
  - the redirect loop's two hops;
  - slow takes at least 3 s (one test);
  - each workspace keeps its own mode;
  - a bad mode or bad `ws` is rejected;
  - a bad `ws` path returns 404;
  - there's no admin route over HTTP.
- **Integration** (`vitest.config.ts` demo stub): extend the stub with an in-memory `getMode`/`setMode`, plus `calls()` that records them for WP-12. New `test/integration/demo-api.test.ts`:
  - GET returns the default and the URL;
  - POST records `setMode`;
  - a bad mode returns 400;
  - `/w/*` on the test demo origin reaches the stub, and on another origin returns 404.
- **Unit:** a `DemoPanel`-free test for the API helpers, if any logic is worth it (keep it small).

### Docs and board (the usual flow)
- **Start:**
  - set the board to 🔄;
  - add a PROMPTS entry (the context entry already appended, plus this one);
  - commit `TASKS: WP-11 started`.
- **TDD:**
  - §7.5 "As built": the dev pass-through and spike result, `/r` behaviour, kv storage, `ws` validation;
  - §7.1: `/api/demo` errors;
  - §12.1: the collapsible panel below `lg`;
  - §14: the stub's `calls()`.
- **Decisions log:** a row for the dev pass-through.
- **Finish:**
  - commit `WP-11: …`;
  - set the board to ✅ (hash, hours vs 1.0);
  - commit `TASKS: WP-11 done`;
  - push `dev`.

## Verification
1. Run `pnpm test` (all three projects), `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` and `pnpm build`.
2. Start `pnpm dev --port 5173 --strictPort`, then check with `curl`:
   - `/w/<ws>/` in each mode (via `POST /api/demo/mode` with the cookie): status, `cache-control`, `set-cookie`, the 302 loop and the 3 s delay;
   - a bad `ws` returns 404.
3. **Local mini-eval** (mock LLM, 0 neurons): for each mode, set it, `POST /api/incidents "what's wrong with my demo service?"`, poll the snapshot and expect the TDD §14 mapping:
   - slow → `high_latency`
   - errors → `origin_5xx`
   - redirect_loop → `redirect_loop`
   - bad_cache → `cache_misconfiguration`
   - healthy → `healthy`
4. **Headless Chromium** (playwright-core in the scratchpad), light and dark, at 1400×900 and 390×844:
   - the panel shows the URL;
   - switching modes persists after a reload;
   - the copy button works;
   - the collapsible works on a phone;
   - the keyboard can reach every radio.

   Look at the screenshots.

```

```
import { routeAgentRequest } from "agents";
import { api } from "./api/router";
import { isId } from "./lib/ids";

export { IncidentAgent } from "./agent/IncidentAgent";
export { DiagnosisWorkflow } from "./workflows/diagnosis";

const notFound = () => new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });

/** `/agents/incident-agent/<32-hex id>` only: other names would create empty Durable Objects. */
function isIncidentPath(request: Request): boolean {
  const [, prefix, agent, name, ...rest] = new URL(request.url).pathname.split("/");
  return prefix === "agents" && agent === "incident-agent" && isId(name) && rest.length === 0;
}

// Only /api/* and /agents/* reach the Worker (run_worker_first); everything else is served from assets.
export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    if (new URL(request.url).pathname.startsWith("/agents/")) {
      // WebSockets only: the app never talks to the agent over plain HTTP.
      const routed = await routeAgentRequest(request, env, {
        onBeforeConnect: (req) => (isIncidentPath(req) ? undefined : notFound()),
        onBeforeRequest: () => notFound(),
      });
      return routed ?? notFound();
    }
    return api.fetch(request, env, ctx);
  },
} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
import type { Context, MiddlewareHandler } from "hono";
import { getCookie, setCookie } from "hono/cookie";
import type { AppEnv } from "../env";
import { isId, newId } from "./ids";

/** Cookie name without its prefix; Hono adds `__Host-`, which forces Secure, Path=/ and no Domain. */
const WS_COOKIE = "ws";
const ONE_YEAR_S = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60;

export function readWorkspaceId(c: Context): string | undefined {
  const value = getCookie(c, WS_COOKIE, "host");
  return isId(value) ? value : undefined;
}

/** The workspace ID from a raw Cookie header: WebSocket upgrades reach the agent without Hono. */
export function workspaceFromCookieHeader(header: string | null): string | undefined {
  const value = /(?:^|;\s*)__Host-ws=([^;]*)/.exec(header ?? "")?.[1];
  return isId(value) ? value : undefined;
}

/**
 * Anonymous workspace identity (FR-8). A well-formed cookie is trusted without a D1 read; a forged ID
 * only creates a fresh, empty workspace. Missing or malformed cookies get a new workspace.
 */
export const workspace: MiddlewareHandler<AppEnv> = async (c, next) => {
  let id = readWorkspaceId(c);
  if (!id) {
    id = newId();
    await c.env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO workspaces (id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)").bind(id, Date.now()).run();
    setCookie(c, WS_COOKIE, id, {
      prefix: "host",
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: true,
      sameSite: "Lax",
      path: "/",
      maxAge: ONE_YEAR_S,
    });
  }
  c.set("workspaceId", id);
  await next();
};
import { applyD1Migrations } from "cloudflare:test";
import { env } from "cloudflare:workers";

await applyD1Migrations(env.DB, env.TEST_MIGRATIONS);
import { env, exports } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const BASE = "https://copilot.example.com";

function wsCookie(res: Response): string | undefined {
  return res.headers.getSetCookie().find((c) => c.startsWith("__Host-ws="));
}

describe("workspace cookie + GET /api/session", () => {
  it("creates a workspace on first visit", async () => {
    const res = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const cookie = wsCookie(res);
    expect(cookie).toMatch(/^__Host-ws=[0-9a-f]{32};/);
    expect(cookie).toContain("Max-Age=31536000");
    expect(cookie).toContain("Path=/");
    expect(cookie).toContain("HttpOnly");
    expect(cookie).toContain("Secure");
    expect(cookie).toContain("SameSite=Lax");
    expect(cookie).not.toContain("Domain");

    const id = cookie!.slice("__Host-ws=".length, "__Host-ws=".length + 32);
    const body = await res.json<{ demoUrl: string; budgetMode: string; limits: Record<string, number> }>();
    expect(body.demoUrl).toBe(`${env.DEMO_BASE_URL}/w/${id}/`);
    expect(body.budgetMode).toBe("ai");
    expect(body.limits).toMatchObject({ runsPerMinute: 3, runsPerHour: 10 });

    const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM workspaces WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first();
    expect(row).toEqual({ id });
  });

  it("keeps an existing workspace without re-setting the cookie", async () => {
    const first = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`);
    const id = wsCookie(first)!.split(";")[0]!.split("=")[1]!;
    const { demoUrl } = await first.json<{ demoUrl: string }>();

    const again = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`, { headers: { cookie: `__Host-ws=${id}` } });
    expect(wsCookie(again)).toBeUndefined();
    expect((await again.json<{ demoUrl: string }>()).demoUrl).toBe(demoUrl);
  });

  it("replaces a malformed cookie", async () => {
    const res = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`, { headers: { cookie: "__Host-ws=not-an-id" } });
    expect(wsCookie(res)).toMatch(/^__Host-ws=[0-9a-f]{32};/);
  });

  it("ignores a cookie without the __Host- prefix", async () => {
    const res = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`, {
      headers: { cookie: "ws=0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef" },
    });
    expect(wsCookie(res)).toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

```
import type { D1Migration } from "cloudflare:test";

declare global {
  namespace Cloudflare {
    interface Env {
      /** Injected by vitest.config.ts for the integration project only. */
      TEST_MIGRATIONS: D1Migration[];
    }
  }
}
import { exports } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const BASE = "https://copilot.example.com";

/** A fresh workspace: its cookie header for later requests. */
async function workspace(): Promise<string> {
  const res = await exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}/api/session`);
  const cookie = res.headers.getSetCookie().find((c) => c.startsWith("__Host-ws="))!;
  return cookie.split(";")[0]!;
}

const post = (path: string, cookie: string, body?: unknown) =>
  exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Cookie: cookie, "content-type": "application/json" },
    ...(body === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  });
const get = (path: string, cookie: string) => exports.default.fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, { headers: { Cookie: cookie } });

describe("/api/incidents", () => {
  it("asks for a target when the message names none", async () => {
    const res = await post("/api/incidents", await workspace(), { message: "everything is broken" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ error: "need_target" });
    expect((await post("/api/incidents", await workspace(), {})).status).toBe(400);
  });

  it("creates an incident for the demo and lists it for its workspace only", async () => {
    const mine = await workspace();
    const res = await post("/api/incidents", mine, { message: "what's wrong with my demo?" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const { id } = await res.json<{ id: string }>();
    expect(id).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/);

    const list = await (await get("/api/incidents", mine)).json<{ id: string; status: string; target: string; unread: boolean }[]>();
    expect(list).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ id, status: "investigating", origin: "chat", unread: false })]);
    expect(list[0]!.target).toMatch(/\/w\/[0-9a-f]{32}\/$/);
    expect(await (await get("/api/incidents", await workspace())).json()).toEqual([]);
  });
```

```
import { CATEGORY_INFO, type Category, type Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";
import { renderTemplate } from "../diagnosis/template";
import type { ToolResult } from "../tools/types";
import { LlmUnavailable, toResult, type ChatCompletion, type LlmRequest, type LlmResult } from "./models";
import type { EvidenceLine } from "./prompts";

/**
 * `LLM_MODE=mock` (TDD §7.4): scripted turns in the recorded chat-completions shape, run through the real
 * mapper, costing 0 neurons. A demo URL doesn't show its mode, so the final turn reads the evidence instead
 * of keying on the target. Test triggers in the question: `#llm-down`, `#llm-invalid`.
 */
export const MOCK_MODEL = "mock";

const LINE = /^\[([a-z]+-\d+)\] (\w+) \((\w+)\): (.*)\n {2}(.*)$/gm;

export function mockCall(req: LlmRequest): LlmResult {
  const user = req.messages.map((m) => m.content).join("\n");
  if (user.includes("#llm-down")) throw new LlmUnavailable("failed", "mock: #llm-down");
  if (req.tools?.length) return toResult(MOCK_MODEL, planningTurn(req.system, user));
  return { text: narration(user), toolCalls: [], usage: { in: 0, out: 0, neurons: 0 }, model: MOCK_MODEL, fallback: false };
}

export function mockStream(req: LlmRequest, onDelta: (text: string) => void): LlmResult {
  const result = mockCall(req);
  const text = result.text ?? "";
  const size = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(text.length / 4));
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += size) onDelta(text.slice(i, i + size));
  return result;
}

function planningTurn(system: string, user: string): ChatCompletion {
  if (user.includes("#llm-invalid")) return completion([{ name: "http_probe", args: "{not json" }]);
  const evidence = parseEvidence(user);
  if (evidence.length === 0) {
    const target = /^Target: (\S+)$/m.exec(system)?.[1] ?? "https://example.com/";
    return completion([
      { name: "dns_lookup", args: JSON.stringify({ hostname: new URL(target).hostname }) },
      { name: "http_probe", args: JSON.stringify({ url: target }) },
    ]);
  }
  const category = pickCategory(evidence);
  const ids = [last(evidence, "http_probe"), last(evidence, "dns_lookup")].flatMap((e) => (e ? [e.evidenceId] : []));
  const findings: Findings = {
    category,
    confidence: category === "inconclusive" ? "low" : "high",
    evidence: ids.length ? ids : [evidence[0]!.evidenceId],
    cause: `Mock diagnosis: ${CATEGORY_INFO[category]}`,
    next_steps: ["Set LLM_MODE=live for a real diagnosis."],
    actions: category === "healthy" ? [] : ["restore_demo_service"],
  };
  return completion([{ name: "submit_findings", args: JSON.stringify(findings) }]);
}

function pickCategory(evidence: EvidenceLine[]): Category {
  const dns = last(evidence, "dns_lookup");
  const http = last(evidence, "http_probe");
  if (dns && /doesn't resolve|No A or AAAA/.test(dns.summary)) return "dns_failure";
  if (http) {
    if (/^(Redirect loop|Too many redirects)/.test(http.summary)) return "redirect_loop";
    if (/^HTTP 5\d\d/.test(http.summary)) return "origin_5xx";
    if (/^Slow:/.test(http.summary)) return "high_latency";
    if (/"asset":\{[^}]*("cacheControl":"[^"]*(no-store|private)|"setCookie":true)/.test(http.digest)) return "cache_misconfiguration";
  }
  if (dns && /^Resolvers disagree/.test(dns.summary)) return "dns_mismatch";
  if (http && /^HTTP [23]\d\d/.test(http.summary)) return "healthy";
  return "inconclusive";
}

/** Four sections from the findings and evidence in the narration prompt, every line cited. */
function narration(user: string): string {
  const json = /^Findings: (.*)$/m.exec(user)?.[1];
  if (!json) return "Mock reply. Set LLM_MODE=live for a real answer.";
  const findings = { actions: [], ...(JSON.parse(json) as Omit<Findings, "actions">) } as Findings;
  const cites = findings.evidence.map((id) => `[${id}]`).join(" ");
  const cause = /\[[a-z]+-\d+\]/.test(findings.cause) ? findings.cause : `${findings.cause.replace(/\.$/, "")} ${cites}.`;
  const results = parseEvidence(user).map((e) => ({ ...e, ok: true, raw: null }) as ToolResult);
  return renderTemplate({ ...findings, cause }, results);
}

export function parseEvidence(text: string): EvidenceLine[] {
```

```
import { Sidebar } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { INCIDENTS_CHANGED, listIncidents, type IncidentRow } from "../lib/api";
import { STATUS_TEXT } from "../lib/incident";

/** The workspace's incidents. Reloads on navigation and on changes; polling and unread dots are WP-19. */
export function IncidentSidebar() {
  const [location] = useLocation();
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<IncidentRow[] | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    const load = () => void listIncidents().then((r) => live && setRows(r));
    load();
    window.addEventListener(INCIDENTS_CHANGED, load);
    return () => {
      live = false;
      window.removeEventListener(INCIDENTS_CHANGED, load);
    };
  }, [location]);

  if (rows === null) return null;
  if (rows.length === 0) return <p className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">No incidents yet.</p>;

  return (
    <Sidebar.Group>
      <Sidebar.GroupLabel>Incidents</Sidebar.GroupLabel>
      <Sidebar.Menu>
        {rows.map((r) => {
          const host = safeHost(r.target);
          return (
            <Sidebar.MenuItem key={r.id}>
              <Sidebar.MenuButton href={`/i/${r.id}`} active={location === `/i/${r.id}`} className="h-auto py-1.5">
                <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
                  <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{host}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{STATUS_TEXT[r.status]}</span>
                </span>
              </Sidebar.MenuButton>
            </Sidebar.MenuItem>
          );
        })}
      </Sidebar.Menu>
    </Sidebar.Group>
  );
}

function safeHost(target: string): string {
  try {
    const u = new URL(target);
    return u.pathname.length > 1 ? `${u.hostname}${u.pathname}` : u.hostname;
  } catch {
    return target;
  }
}
```

</details>

```
import { CodeBlock, Loader, Surface } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { CaretRightIcon, CertificateIcon, GlobeIcon, ListMagnifyingGlassIcon, PulseIcon } from "@phosphor-icons/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { CheckView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import type { ToolName } from "../../worker/tools/types";
import type { Evidence } from "../hooks/useIncident";
import { StatusBadge } from "./StatusBadge";

const ICONS: Record<ToolName, typeof GlobeIcon> = {
  dns_lookup: ListMagnifyingGlassIcon,
  http_probe: GlobeIcon,
  cert_info: CertificateIcon,
  provider_status: PulseIcon,
};

type Props = { check: CheckView; status: CheckView["status"]; getEvidence: (id: string) => Promise<Evidence | null> };

/** One check: tool, evidence ID, status and summary; the raw evidence loads on first expand (TDD §12.2). */
export function CheckCard({ check, status, getEvidence }: Props) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [evidence, setEvidence] = useState<Evidence | null | "loading" | "error">(null);
  const Icon = ICONS[check.tool];
  const finished = status !== "pending" && status !== "running";

  const toggle = async () => {
    const next = !open;
    setOpen(next);
    if (next && (evidence === null || evidence === "error")) {
      setEvidence("loading");
      try {
        setEvidence((await getEvidence(check.evidenceId)) ?? "error");
      } catch {
        setEvidence("error");
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <Surface id={`check-${check.evidenceId}`} tabIndex={-1} className="rounded-lg border border-(--cf-border) outline-none">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3">
        <Icon aria-hidden size={18} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-(--cf-fg-muted)" />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-medium">{check.label}</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{check.evidenceId}</span>
            <StatusBadge status={status} />
          </div>
          {check.summary && <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs break-words text-(--cf-fg-muted)">{check.summary}</p>}
        </div>
        {finished && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void toggle()}
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls={`evidence-${check.evidenceId}`}
            className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-xs text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange"
          >
            <CaretRightIcon aria-hidden size={12} className={open ? "rotate-90 transition-transform" : "transition-transform"} />
            Evidence
          </button>
```

ERROR_TEXT.demo_unavailable : SAVE_FAILED);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <section aria-labelledby=\"demo-panel-title\" className=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">\n      <div>\n        <h2 id=\"demo-panel-title\" className=\"font-display text-base font-medium\">\n          Demo service\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">Break your own demo site, then ask the copilot what's wrong.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      {demo && (\n        <>\n          <Radio.Group<DemoMode> legend=\"Mode\" value={demo.mode} onValueChange={(m) => void change(m)} disabled={saving}>\n            {DEMO_MODES.map((m) => (\n              <Radio.Item<DemoMode> key={m} value={m} label={DEMO_MODE_LABELS[m]} />\n            ))}\n          </Radio.Group>\n\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Demo URL</span>\n            <ClipboardText text={demo.url} className=\"w-full min-w-0\" tooltip={{ text: \"Copy URL\", copiedText: \"Copied\" }} />\n            <a\n              href={demo.url}\n              target=\"_blank\"\n              rel=\"noreferrer\"\n              className=\"flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-md text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange\"\n            >\n              Open the demo\n              <ArrowSquareOutIcon aria-hidden size={14} />\n            </a>\n          </div>\n\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">\n            Then ask: <q className=\"text-(--cf-fg)\">What's wrong with my demo service?</q>\n          </p>\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      {error && (\n        <p role=\"alert\" className=\"text-sm text-(--cf-fg-error,var(--cf-fg))\">\n          {error}\n        </p>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\nEOF\ngrep -n \"cf-fg-error\\|--cf-\" web/styles.css | head -30",

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createIncident, listIncidents } from "../../../web/lib/api";
import { otherHost, runNote } from "../../../web/lib/incident";
import { parseInline, parseMarkdown } from "../../../web/lib/markdown";
import type { TimelineView } from "../../../worker/agent/state";

const known = new Set(["http-1", "dns-1"]);

describe("parseMarkdown", () => {
  it("handles the narration format", () => {
    const md = "**Likely cause:** The origin returns HTTP 500 [http-1].\n\n**Evidence**\n- HTTP probe: 500/500/500 [http-1]\n- DNS lookup: ok [dns-1]\n\n_1 uncited statement removed_";
    expect(parseMarkdown(md, known)).toEqual([
      { t: "p", c: [{ t: "bold", c: [{ t: "text", v: "Likely cause:" }] }, { t: "text", v: " The origin returns HTTP 500 " }, { t: "ref", id: "http-1" }, { t: "text", v: "." }] },
      { t: "p", c: [{ t: "bold", c: [{ t: "text", v: "Evidence" }] }] },
      {
        t: "ul",
        items: [
          [{ t: "text", v: "HTTP probe: 500/500/500 " }, { t: "ref", id: "http-1" }],
          [{ t: "text", v: "DNS lookup: ok " }, { t: "ref", id: "dns-1" }],
        ],
    expect(runNote([ev(1, "run_finished", "done")])).toBeNull();
    expect(runNote([ev(1, "run_finished", "llm_unavailable")])).toBeNull();
    expect(runNote([ev(1, "run_finished", "limit_tools"), ev(2, "run_started")])).toBeNull();
    expect(runNote([])).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("api", () => {
  const fake = (status: number, body: unknown) => (async () => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status })) as unknown as typeof fetch;
  it("maps responses to results and errors", async () => {
    expect(await createIncident("x", fake(200, { id: "abc" }))).toEqual({ ok: true, id: "abc" });
    expect(await createIncident("x", fake(400, { error: "need_target" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "need_target" });
    expect(await createIncident("x", fake(503, { error: "demo_unavailable" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "demo_unavailable" });
    expect(await createIncident("x", fake(429, { retryAfter: 30 }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "rate_limited" });
    expect(await createIncident("x", fake(500, { error: "boom" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "server" });
    const offline = (async () => {
      throw new TypeError("Failed to fetch");
    }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
    expect(await createIncident("x", offline)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "network" });
    expect(await listIncidents(offline)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```
| Demo (`@cloudflare/vitest-plugin`, demo Worker) | `demo.test.ts` | Every mode's status, headers and timing, through `exports.default` and the `DemoAdmin` RPC |

- **Projects** (`vitest.config.ts`): `unit` runs in node. `integration` loads `wrangler.jsonc` with `LLM_MODE=mock` and a valid test `DEMO_BASE_URL`, and with `remoteBindings: false`, so the AI binding never goes remote: tests need no login and spend no neurons. `demo` loads `demo-service/wrangler.jsonc`.
- **Fake internet (WP-09):** the integration project's `outboundService` is an inline `fake-internet` Worker. It answers DoH for `*.example.com` (NXDOMAIN for `nxdomain.example.com`), returns 500 for `broken.example.com` and serves HTML for other `*.example.com` hosts, so no integration test touches the real network. `diagnosis.test.ts` runs real local Workflows through `introspectWorkflow`; `plan.test.ts` covers every limit with a fake `io`.
- Unit tests load the recorded fixtures as JSON imports and the `.sse` files with Vite `?raw` imports (`test/unit/raw.d.ts`), because the worker tsconfig has no Node types.
- **Demo stub:** the test runtime won't start while a service binding points at a Worker it doesn't know about. So `integration` adds a small inline stand-in `incident-copilot-demo` Worker behind `DEMO`, and `approval.test.ts` asserts what the main Worker asks it to do (e.g. `setMode`). The real demo service is tested only in `demo`.
- Tests use `env` and `exports` from `cloudflare:workers` (the plugin's `cloudflare:test` `env`/`SELF` are deprecated). Workflow tests use the plugin's Workflow introspection APIs. Retrying events with `waitForEvent` and the timeout path are verified in WP-02.
```

### 🤖 Claude · 17:12:41

WP-11 is finished and pushed to `dev` (`09398c0`, board update `6a8b141`). It took about 0.3 h against the 1.0 h estimate, and all 460 tests pass, along with typecheck, lint and build.

**What you get**
- **Demo site:** it can be put into any of the five modes (healthy, slow, 500 errors, redirect loop, bad caching). Each workspace has its own mode, so reviewers don't affect each other. Mode changes only go through the private connection from the main app; there's no public way to change them.
- **Demo panel:** it sits in the right column. You pick a mode, then copy or open the demo URL. On a phone it becomes a closed "Demo service" section at the top of the page.
- **Checked end to end locally:** I set each mode and asked "what's wrong with my demo service?". All five came back with the right diagnosis (slow → high latency, errors → server errors, and so on). In the browser:
  - the mode stays set after a reload;
  - the demo page really changes;
  - arrow keys switch modes;
  - copying the URL works.

  I checked this in light and dark, and at phone width.

**One design decision.** When run locally, the demo site doesn't get its own address. So the main app passes `/w/...` requests through to it, but only when the demo URL points back at the app itself, which is only true locally. Once deployed, the demo has its own address and this pass-through stays switched off. `.dev.vars` now points the demo URL at `http://localhost:5173`. This is written up in the TDD and the decisions log.

**Things you should know**
1. **Your local `.dev.vars` says `LLM_MODE=live`, not `mock` as the handoff said.** So my 12 test runs used the real model and spent neurons. Today's local estimate is 2,656 of the 10,000 free daily neurons, and my runs are part of that. I left the setting alone in case you set it on purpose. Should I switch it back to `mock`?
2. **The live model only suggested the fix ("restore the demo service") for 500 errors.** For slow, redirect loop and bad caching it suggested nothing. The scoring script (TDD §14) expects that suggestion every time, so I've noted it as a risk on the board to handle in WP-12.

PR #3 is still open from `dev` to `main`, so these commits are now part of it too. Next up is **WP-12**: approving the fix, re-checking the site, and the `ApprovalCard`.

Try again.\";\nconst NONE = \"none\";\ntype Choice = DemoProblem | typeof NONE;\n\n/**\n * \"Break the demo service\" panel (FR-33, TDD §12.1). One main problem at a time (they change how the page\n * answers), plus an optional slow-down on top, so every combination stays visible to the diagnosis.\n */\nexport function DemoPanel() {\n  const [demo, setDemo] = useState<Demo | null>(null);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let live = true;\n    const load = () =>\n      void getDemo().then((r) => {\n        if (!live) return;\n        if (r.ok) {\n          setDemo(r.demo);\n          setError(null);\n        } else setError(ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);\n      });\n    load();\n    window.addEventListener(DEMO_CHANGED, load);\n    return () => {\n      live = false;\n      window.removeEventListener(DEMO_CHANGED, load);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const problem = DEMO_PROBLEMS.find((p) => demo?.modes.includes(p)) ?? null;\n  const slow = demo?.modes.includes(\"slow\") ?? false;\n\n  const change = async (nextProblem: DemoProblem | null, nextSlow: boolean) => {\n    if (!demo) return;\n    const before = demo;\n    const modes = modesFrom(nextProblem, nextSlow);\n    setDemo({ ...demo, modes });\n    setSaving(true);\n    const r = await setDemoModes(modes);\n    setSaving(false);\n    if (r.ok) {\n      setDemo(r.demo);\n      setError(null);\n    } else {\n      setDemo(before);\n      setError(r.error === \"demo_unavailable\" ? ERROR_TEXT.demo_unavailable : SAVE_FAILED);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <section aria-labelledby=\"demo-panel-title\" className=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">\n      <div>\n        <h2 id=\"demo-panel-title\" className=\"font-display text-base font-medium\">\n          Demo service\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">Break your own demo site, then ask the copilot what's wrong.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      {demo && (\n        <>\n          <p className=\"text-sm\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n            <span className=\"text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">Now: </span>\n            <span className=\"font-medium\">{demo.modes.map((m) => DEMO_MODE_LABELS[m]).join(\" + \")}</span>\n          </p>\n\n          <Radio.Group<Choice>\n            legend=\"Problem\"\n            value={problem ?? NONE}\n            onValueChange={(c) => void change(c === NONE ? null : c, slow)}\n            disabled={saving}\n          >\n            <Radio.Item<Choice> value={NONE} label=\"None\" />\n            {DEMO_PROBLEMS.map((p) => (\n              <Radio.Item<Choice> key={p} value={p} label={DEMO_MODE_LABELS[p]} />\n            ))}\n          </Radio.Group>\n\n          <Checkbox label=\"Also slow (+3 s per response)\" checked={slow} disabled={saving} onCheckedChange={(c) => void change(problem, c === true)} />\n\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5\">\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Demo URL</span>\n            <ClipboardText text={demo.url} className=\"w-full min-w-0\" tooltip={{ text: \"Copy URL\", copiedText: \"Copied\" }} />\n            <a\n              href={demo.url}\n              target=\"_blank\"\n              rel=\"noreferrer\"\n              className=\"flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-md text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted) hover:text-(--cf-fg) focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cf-orange\"\n            >\n              Open the demo\n              <ArrowSquareOutIcon aria-hidden size={14} />\n            </a>\n          </div>\n\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)\">\n            Then ask: <q className=\"text-(--cf-fg)\">What's wrong with my demo service?</q>\n          </p>\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      {error && (\n        <div role=\"alert\">\n          <Banner variant=\"error\" description={error} />\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\nEOF\npnpm -s typecheck 2>&1 | head -20",
```json
  "description": "Update web API helpers and rebuild DemoPanel with problem radio + slow checkbox"
}
```

### 🤖 Claude · 17:30:35

The demo can now combine modes, in the way you chose: **one main problem plus an optional slow-down**. It's pushed to `dev`: the code is in `e891c6c` and the doc updates in `f11d904`. 466 tests pass, and typecheck, lint and build are clean.

**How it works**
- **Allowed:** Healthy on its own, Slow on its own, or one of 500 errors, Redirect loop or Bad caching, each with or without Slow.
- **Refused:** any set that includes Healthy plus anything else, or two main problems together. The server rejects these with `invalid_modes`, so they can't be set even by calling the API directly.
- **Panel:** a "Problem" choice (None, 500 errors, Redirect loop, Bad caching), an "Also slow (+3 s per response)" checkbox, and a "Now:" line showing the current combination, for example "Slow (+3 s) + 500 errors".
- **One change from the preview I showed you:** "Also slow" stays usable when the problem is "None". Otherwise "slow on its own" couldn't be chosen, and the eval needs it.
- **For WP-12:** the "restore the demo" fix will clear every mode at once.

**Checked:** in the browser, combinations stay set after a reload, and switching the problem keeps "slow" on. I checked light and dark, at desktop and phone widths. I also ran a local diagnosis on each "problem + slow" combination. That used the mock model, so it cost nothing; I saw you'd switched `.dev.vars` back to `mock`.

**Decision for you:**
- **slow + 500 errors** is diagnosed as server errors, and **slow + redirect loop** as a redirect loop, as intended. In both, the slow timings show in the evidence.
- **slow + bad caching** is diagnosed as **high latency**, not bad caching, because the rules check speed before caching.

I'd keep it that way. On a real website a 3-second page is the more urgent problem, and putting caching first would hide slowness on the many real sites that have uncacheable files. If you'd rather the demo always name the main problem, I can swap the order of those two checks.
