## 6. Data model

### 6.1 D1 (`migrations/0001_init.sql`), the full schema from day one

```sql
CREATE TABLE workspaces (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE incidents_index (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL, target TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL, origin TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat',          -- chat | watch
  unread INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX idx_incidents_ws ON incidents_index(workspace_id, updated_at DESC);
CREATE TABLE watches (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL, url TEXT NOT NULL,
  interval_sec INTEGER NOT NULL, next_run INTEGER NOT NULL, expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  last_status TEXT, consecutive_passes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, open_incident_id TEXT);
CREATE INDEX idx_watches_due ON watches(next_run);
CREATE TABLE webhooks (workspace_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, kind TEXT NOT NULL, url_ciphertext BLOB NOT NULL,
  iv BLOB NOT NULL, url_hint TEXT NOT NULL, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL);   -- url_hint = masked display
CREATE TABLE llm_usage (day TEXT PRIMARY KEY, neurons_est INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0);
CREATE TABLE usage_counters (workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, window_start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, PRIMARY KEY (workspace_id, kind, window_start));  -- kind: run | voice
```

Consistency: the DO is the source of truth. `incidents_index` is upserted after every committed transition and on `onStart` (reconcile).

### 6.2 IncidentAgent SQLite (`agent/schema.sql.ts`)

```sql
incident(id PK, workspace_id, target, origin, status, category, confidence, mode, created_at, updated_at)
messages(id PK, run_id, role, content, evidence_refs_json, created_at)          -- role: user|assistant|system
runs(id PK, seq, kind, workflow_id, status, started_at, finished_at, findings_json, tool_calls, llm_turns)
checks(evidence_id PK, run_id, tool, args_json, status, summary, digest_json, result_json, started_at, finished_at)
approvals(id PK, run_id, action_id, args_json, status, workflow_id, created_at, decided_at, outcome_json)
           -- status: pending|approved|rejected|expired|superseded
timeline(id INTEGER PK AUTOINCREMENT, ts, event_type, detail_json)
monitor(id PK CHECK(id=1), kind, ticks_left, consecutive_passes, schedule_id)      -- kind: post_action|burst
postmortem(id PK CHECK(id=1), markdown, generated_at)
```

*WP-08 additions:*
- `incident.watch_id` and `incident.unread` (mirrored to the index).
- `runs.question` and `runs.mode`. `runs.status` is one of `running`, `done`, `limit_turns`, `limit_tools`, `limit_deadline`, `limit_budget`, `llm_unavailable` or `failed` (§8.2).
- `checks.turn` and `checks.seq` with `UNIQUE(run_id, turn, seq)`, so a retried planning step gets the same evidence IDs back.
- Lists are ordered by insertion (`rowid`).

### 6.3 Synced state (`agent/state.ts`), kept small (full evidence goes through `getEvidence`)

```ts
type IncidentStatus = "investigating" | "awaiting_approval" | "mitigated" | "resolved";
type Category = "dns_failure" | "dns_mismatch" | "tls_expiry" | "origin_5xx" | "edge_error" | "high_latency"
  | "redirect_loop" | "cache_misconfiguration" | "provider_outage" | "healthy" | "inconclusive";
type Confidence = "high" | "medium" | "low";

interface IncidentState {
  id: string; target: string; origin: "chat" | "watch";
  status: IncidentStatus; category: Category | null; confidence: Confidence | null;
  mode: "ai" | "rule_based";            // mode of the latest run
  activeRunId: string | null;           // non-null → composer disabled (D13)
  checks: CheckView[];                  // ≤ 40
  messages: MessageView[];              // last 50 (stream deltas arrive separately)
  approvals: ApprovalView[];            // latest 5
  timeline: TimelineView[];             // last 100
  monitor: { kind: "post_action" | "burst"; passes: number; ticksLeft: number } | null;
  hasPostmortem: boolean;
}
interface CheckView { evidenceId: string; tool: ToolName; label: string;
  status: "pending" | "running" | "pass" | "fail" | "error" | "unavailable"; summary: string;
  startedAt: number; finishedAt?: number }
// WS side-channel (broadcast, not state): {type:"stream_start", messageId} | {type:"delta", messageId, text} | {type:"stream_end", messageId}
```

*WP-08:*
- The state also carries the latest run's `findings` (for the approval card's "why").
- `CheckView` has a `runId`.
- Timeline `detail` values are flat scalars: a recursive JSON type makes the RPC stub types too deep to check.
- `stream_start` resets the client buffer when a retried narration streams again.
- `syncState()` rebuilds the state from SQL after every change, and `validateStateChange` rejects every client-originated write.

### 6.4 IDs

Incident, workspace and approval IDs are 128-bit random hex values (`crypto.getRandomValues`). Evidence IDs are `<tool>-<n>`, with prefixes `dns`, `http`, `cert` and `status`, and n is unique per incident. Workflow IDs are `diag-<incidentId>-<seq>`, `watch-<watchId>-<epochMinute>` and `notify-<incidentId>-<status>`.

## 7. Interfaces
### 7.6 Action catalog (`actions/catalog.ts`)

| ID | Args | Proposable when | Execute | Verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `restore_demo_service` | `{}` | Target host = demo host, path starts with `/w/<incident.workspaceId>/`, category ≠ `healthy` | `env.DEMO.setModes(ws, ["healthy"])` (clears every mode) | `quickCheck` pass, then `afterAction` |
| `add_to_watchlist` | `{intervalSec: 300}` | Not already watched and under caps | Insert into `watches` | None; outcome goes on the timeline |
| `recheck_burst` | `{}` | No monitor active | Agent `monitor(kind:"burst")` | Ticks |
| `generate_postmortem` | `{}` | status = `resolved` | `generatePostmortem()` | None |

The Workflow drops any action that isn't proposable. At most one action per run.

## 8. Core flows

### 8.1 New incident (chat)

1. `POST /api/incidents {message}`. The Worker parses the target (D9), checks the run quota (D14, from WP-18), creates the ID, and calls `agent.init(...)`.
2. `init` writes the incident, message and timeline `opened` rows, upserts the index, and calls `startRun(message, "initial")`.
3. `startRun` makes sure `activeRunId` is null (otherwise 409), then calls `this.runWorkflow("DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW", {incidentId, runId, kind}, {id: "diag-<id>-<seq>"})` and sets `activeRunId`.
4. The client navigates to `/i/:id` and runs `useAgent({ agent: "incident-agent", name: id })`. State arrives right away.

### 8.2 DiagnosisWorkflow.run

```
ctx = step.do("context", agent.getRunContext)                 // incl. budget mode
if ctx.mode == rule_based → RULE_PATH
for turn in 1..MAX_PLAN_TURNS (while deadline/budget/tool-cap allow):
  r = step.do(`llm-turn-${turn}`, {retries:{limit:2,backoff:"exponential"}}, () => plan(ctx, evidence))
      // inside: callLlm → validate calls (zod + origin pin) → agent.addPendingChecks
  on LlmUnavailable → RULE_PATH (mode=rule_based)
  on invalid call: 1st → feed error back next turn; 2nd → RULE_PATH
  for call in r.toolCalls: step.do(`tool-${evidenceId}`, retries, () => run tool → agent.recordCheck)
  if r.findings → break
findings = r.findings ?? classify(evidence)                    // rules fill gaps (D5)
step.do("findings", agent.setFindings)                         // category/confidence/transitions
step.do("narrate",  agent.narrate)                             // streams (D4)
action = firstProposable(findings.actions)
if action:
  { approvalId } = step.do("propose", agent.proposeAction)     // → awaiting_approval; supersede (D13)
  if !approvalId return                                        // deduped
  try   await this.waitForApproval(step, { timeout: "24 hours" })
  catch WorkflowRejectedError → step.do("rejected", agent.recordApprovalOutcome); return
  catch timeout → step.do("expired", ...); return              // "no action taken" → investigating
  out = step.do("action", () => execute(action))              // idempotent (setModes ["healthy"] is)
  v   = step.do("verify", () => quickCheck(target))
  step.do("after-action", () => agent.afterAction(runId, v))   // mitigated + monitor (D11)
step.do("finish", agent.finishRun)                            // clears activeRunId
RULE_PATH: run dns, http, cert, provider (those not already run) → classify → template narrative → same tail
```

Tool steps retry with `{limit: 2}` because each retry spends subrequest budget. LLM steps have the model fallback inside the step. `finishRun` runs before the approval wait, so the input is enabled again while waiting (D13).

**Limits keep progress (user requirement, 2026-10-15).** Hitting any limit ends the run with the best diagnosis from what was already found. It never throws work away or fails the run. The limits are turns, tool calls, the 60 s deadline, the subrequest budget, `max_tokens`, the neuron allocation and the LLM being unavailable.
- **Evidence is durable as it lands.** Each check is stored by `recordCheck` in its own step, so nothing gathered is lost to a later failure, and the rule path never re-runs a check that already has a result.
- **Turn or tool cap:** the last planning turn offers only `submit_findings` (`llmTools({finishOnly: true})`), so the model sums up what it has. If the tool budget runs out early, that finish turn comes next, as long as the LLM is available and budget is left.
- **Deadline, subrequest budget, `LlmUnavailable`, allocation:** go straight to findings. Use `classify` over all evidence gathered so far, merged with any usable part of the model's last `submit_findings` through `salvageFindings` (`loop/findings.ts`). The rule path only runs missing checks if budget is left; otherwise it classifies what exists.
- **Invalid or cut-off `submit_findings`** (bad field, sentence evidence, `truncated` JSON): after the one feedback retry (FR-12), `salvageFindings` keeps the model's valid fields and fills the rest from the rules.
- **Narration cut short** (`LlmUnavailable.partial`, or `truncated`): keep the streamed text if `isWellFormedNarration` passes, run `checkCitations` on it, and add "_Narration was cut short._". Otherwise store `renderTemplate`. Text the user already saw is never silently replaced by something unrelated.
- **The run records why it stopped** (`runs.status`: `done` · `limit_turns` · `limit_tools` · `limit_deadline` · `limit_budget` · `llm_unavailable`), plus a timeline event. The UI says which limit hit and how many checks the diagnosis uses.

**As built (WP-09):**
- **Driver + `io`.** All the run logic is the pure `loop/plan.ts` `diagnose(io, runId, limits)`. `workflows/diagnosis.ts` only supplies `io`: `step` = `step.do` with `{limit: 2, delay: "1 second", backoff: "exponential"}`, the agent RPC, `callLlm`, `runTool` with global `fetch` and `devAllowOrigin(env)`. `io.now()` is only called inside steps (each step returns `at`), so time-based decisions replay the same way after a restart.
- **Step names:** `context` · `llm-turn-N` · `pending-N` · `tool-<evidenceId>` · `pending-rules` · `rules` · `findings` · `narrate` · `finish` (`finish-failed` on an unexpected error).
- **Budget.** It's rebuilt from step results: 1 per agent RPC, 1 or 2 per LLM turn (fallback), each tool's real count plus its `recordCheck`. A tool's worst-case cap is reserved before it runs, and 4 are held back from the start for the tail.
- **Statuses.** A finish-only turn is forced by the turn cap (the last turn), the tool cap, or not enough budget for another tool plus a turn. Its status is `limit_turns` / `limit_tools` / `limit_budget` even when the model then submits good findings. Once the deadline passes there are **no more LLM turns**: rules + salvage, `limit_deadline`. A second invalid turn goes to the rules with status `done`.
- **Rule path.** It runs only the baseline checks (`dns_lookup` host, `http_probe` target) that this run hasn't done, if budget and time allow. Then the agent's `ruleFindings(runId, lastFindingsArgs)` = `classify` over all stored checks + `salvageFindings` (raw evidence never leaves the DO).
- **Findings** must cite evidence IDs that exist; unknown IDs are dropped, and none left counts as invalid. Calls past the tool cap are dropped and the model is told.
- **A check that throws** is stored as an `error` check. A tool step that fails even after retries is counted at its cap and the run continues.
- **`finishRun`** marks checks that were planned but never run as `unavailable` ("Not run: the run stopped before this check.").
- **Launch.** `startRun` → `launchRun(runId)` (idempotent, ID `diag-<incidentId>-<seq>`, stored in `runs.workflow_id`). If the Workflow can't start, the run finishes `failed` with a system message. Test seam: `DIAGNOSIS_AUTOSTART=off` (integration config only) skips the auto-launch so agent tests can drive runs by hand.
- `PLAN_MAX_TOKENS` = 1500, because gpt's reasoning shares the budget.
- **Approval tail:** not built yet. WP-12 inserts propose → wait → act → verify after `narrate` (marked in `plan.ts`).

**`waitForApproval` behaviour (WP-02, local Workflows, `agents` 0.28.1):**
- **Approve:** returns the `metadata` passed to `approveWorkflow`.
- **Reject:** throws `WorkflowRejectedError` ("Workflow rejected: <reason>").
- **Timeout:** throws a plain `Error` ("Execution timed out after <ms> ms"). It can be caught in `run()`, and the Workflow carries on to later steps. The catch rule is: `WorkflowRejectedError` → rejected, **any other error from the wait → expired**. Don't match on the message text, which may differ in production.
- **Late or repeated calls:** calling `approveWorkflow` after the timeout, or a second time, doesn't throw. D13's idempotency is still enforced through the `approvals` table.
- **The SDK's built-in tracking (`getWorkflow`) isn't a source of truth:** a rejection calls `step.reportError` and shows `errored` even though the run continues, and other runs stay `queued` unless `step.reportComplete` is called. The UI reads the `approvals` and `runs` tables only.
- Inside `AgentWorkflow.run()`, the instance ID is **`this.workflowId`** (or `event.instanceId`). The documented `this.instanceId` is `undefined` in 0.28.1.

### 8.3 Approval

1. The client calls `approve(approvalId)` (owner only).
2. If the approval is already decided, nothing happens. Otherwise it's marked approved and the Agent calls `this.approveWorkflow(workflowId)`.
3. The Workflow resumes.
4. Reject calls `rejectWorkflow`. A supersede calls `rejectWorkflow(..., {reason: "superseded"})`.

### 8.4 Monitoring (D11), in `agent/monitor.ts`

1. `afterAction(pass)` sets passes = 1, status = `mitigated`, and schedules `monitorTick` every 15 s (10 ticks at most).
2. Each tick runs `quickCheck`. A pass adds one to the count (3 passes → `resolved`). A fail resets the count (`mitigated` → `investigating`).
3. When the ticks run out, the incident stays `mitigated`.
4. Bursts work the same way at 60 s × 15.
5. For watch incidents, `reportWatchCheck` from cron feeds the same counter. In that case a pass while `investigating` moves to `mitigated` (recovered without action).

### 8.5 Watch (cron, D12)

1. `scheduled()` runs `SELECT * FROM watches WHERE next_run <= now AND expires_at > now ORDER BY next_run LIMIT 6`.
2. For each watch, it runs `quickCheck` with a subrequest counter (stopping before 50) and updates `next_run`, `last_status` and `consecutive_passes`.
3. If `open_incident_id` is set, it calls `agent.reportWatchCheck(pass)`.
4. If the check fails and no incident is open, it calls `WATCH_WORKFLOW.create({id: "watch-<id>-<minute>"})`. Duplicate IDs are ignored.
5. Expired watches are deleted.

`WatchWorkflow`:
1. `step.sleep("30 seconds")`, then a re-check, done twice (2 of 3 failures confirm).
2. If not confirmed, end.
3. Otherwise `step.do("open")` creates an incident ID, calls `agent.init({origin: "watch", watchId})` and sets `watches.open_incident_id`. `init` starts the diagnosis.
4. `step.do("notify")` creates `NOTIFY_WORKFLOW` with `notify-<id>-opened`.

The Agent creates a NotifyWorkflow for watch incidents when they transition to mitigated or resolved (D18). When an incident resolves, the watch's `open_incident_id` is cleared.

### 8.6 Follow-up

`sendMessage(text)` checks the role, then `activeRunId` (409 if set), then the quota, then `startRun(text, "followup")`. The context includes earlier evidence digests, and the model may call 0 tools before `submit_findings`. A hostname in the text that's outside the origin pin gets a system message with a "New incident for X" button.

## 10. State machine (`agent/transitions.ts`)

| From | To | Trigger |
|---|---|---|
| (new) | `investigating` | `init` (chat message or confirmed watch failure) |
| `investigating` | `awaiting_approval` | `proposeAction` created a card |
| `investigating` | `resolved` | Findings `healthy` on the first run of a chat incident, or a manual resolve |
| `awaiting_approval` | `investigating` | Rejected or expired |
| `awaiting_approval` | `mitigated` | `afterAction` with a verify pass |
| `awaiting_approval` | `investigating` | `afterAction` with a verify fail |
| `investigating` | `mitigated` | A monitor or watch pass without an action |
| `mitigated` | `resolved` | 3 passes in a row |
| `mitigated` | `investigating` | A failure comes back |
| any non-resolved | `resolved` | Manual `resolve()` (FR-7) |

`transition(to, reason)` throws on an illegal edge. Every transition writes a timeline row, a `setState` update, an index upsert, and a NotifyWorkflow for watch incidents.

## 11. Safety implementation

```
10:| D4 | **Streaming:** the final Workflow step `step.do("narrate")` calls the Agent over RPC (`narrate(runId)`). The Agent calls the LLM with `stream: true`, sends deltas over the WebSocket, and stores the final text. It's idempotent per `runId` and counts as LLM turn 4. | Real streaming that stays durable (FR-3) |
12:| D6 | **Tools are pinned to the incident's origin.** `http_probe` accepts any path on the incident host. `dns_lookup` and `cert_info` accept the host or CNAME targets found in this incident's DNS evidence. `provider_status` uses a fixed list. A different host needs a new incident, and the UI offers a button. | Prevents use as an open proxy or a prompt-injection pivot |
14:| D8 | **Incident links are view-only for non-owners.** Connections are tagged `owner` or `viewer` from the cookie in `onConnect`. Every mutating callable requires `owner`. | Stops quota theft and approvals from someone who only has a link |
17:| D11 | Dev: **post-diagnosis monitoring runs on Agent scheduler ticks** (`this.schedule`), not inside Workflows. That covers the 15 s ticks after an action, the 60 s ticks for `recheck_burst`, and watch cron results feeding the same counter. Workflows end after verify (Diagnosis) or notify (Watch). | The 50-subrequest limit per Workflow run on Free |
19:| D13 | **One planning run and one pending approval per incident.** The input is disabled while a run is active, and the server returns 409 for a second send. Follow-ups are allowed while an approval is pending. A different new proposal **supersedes** the pending one (`rejectWorkflow(old, "superseded")`). The same proposal is deduplicated. Approve and Reject are idempotent. | No conflicting actions and no piling up of cards |
24:| D18 | Dev: **webhooks fire only for watch-originated incidents** (opened, mitigated, resolved). On-demand incidents have the user present, so the in-app channel covers them. Sending happens in `NotifyWorkflow`, whose instance ID `notify-<incidentId>-<state>` makes it at most once per state change. | FR-54 dedupe comes from the Workflow ID being unique |
128:| FR-13 | Each LLM call and each tool call is a separate durable `step.do` with retries. | Must |
129:| FR-14 | **Deterministic fallback:** if the model errors, loops or exceeds bounds, run all four checks and produce a rule-based summary. | Must |
130:| FR-15 | Every factual claim in a diagnosis cites at least one evidence ID; the UI links references to check cards. | Must |
131:| FR-16 | The diagnosis is classified into a failure category (§6.4) and includes a confidence level (high/medium/low). | Should |
136:| FR-20 | The agent may only **propose** actions; nothing runs without a human Approve click. | Must |
137:| FR-21 | Approval is implemented with Workflows `waitForEvent`, with a **24-hour timeout** that records "no action taken". | Must |
138:| FR-22 | Action catalog: `restore_demo_service` (demo target only), `add_to_watchlist`, `recheck_burst` (every minute for 15 minutes), `generate_postmortem`. | Must (restore_demo, add_to_watchlist); Should (rest) |
139:| FR-23 | After an action, the Workflow re-verifies with fresh checks and records the outcome on the timeline. | Must |
140:| FR-24 | No action can target infrastructure outside the app's own control. | Must |
202:- **Deterministic fallback (FR-14):** run all 4 checks, then apply rule-based classification (§6.4) and a template summary. The UI shows "AI unavailable, rule-based diagnosis".
208:- The model can only **propose** action IDs from the catalog (FR-22). The Workflow validates them before showing an approval card.
```

import type { IncidentStatus } from "./state";

/** Legal status edges (TDD §10). Every non-resolved status can be resolved by hand (FR-7). */
const EDGES: Record<IncidentStatus, readonly IncidentStatus[]> = {
  investigating: ["awaiting_approval", "mitigated", "resolved"],
  awaiting_approval: ["investigating", "mitigated", "resolved"],
  mitigated: ["investigating", "resolved"],
  resolved: [],
};

export class IllegalTransition extends Error {
  override name = "IllegalTransition";
}

export function canTransition(from: IncidentStatus, to: IncidentStatus): boolean {
  return EDGES[from].includes(to);
}

export function assertTransition(from: IncidentStatus, to: IncidentStatus): void {
  if (!canTransition(from, to)) throw new IllegalTransition(`Illegal transition ${from} → ${to}`);
}
/**
 * IncidentAgent SQLite (TDD §6.2), created in `onStart`. Additions to the TDD sketch:
 * `incident.watch_id`/`unread`, `runs.question`/`mode`, and `checks.turn`/`seq` with a unique key so
 * `addPendingChecks` returns the same evidence IDs when a Workflow step retries.
 */
export const SCHEMA = [
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS incident (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL, target TEXT NOT NULL,
    origin TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, category TEXT, confidence TEXT, mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ai',
    watch_id TEXT, unread INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT, role TEXT NOT NULL, content TEXT NOT NULL,
    evidence_refs_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]', created_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, seq INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    question TEXT NOT NULL, workflow_id TEXT, status TEXT NOT NULL, mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ai',
    started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, finished_at INTEGER, findings_json TEXT, tool_calls INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    llm_turns INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS checks (evidence_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT NOT NULL, turn INTEGER NOT NULL,
    seq INTEGER NOT NULL, tool TEXT NOT NULL, args_json TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, summary TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    digest TEXT, raw_json TEXT, started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, finished_at INTEGER, UNIQUE (run_id, turn, seq))`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS approvals (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT NOT NULL, action_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    args_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', status TEXT NOT NULL, workflow_id TEXT, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    decided_at INTEGER, outcome_json TEXT)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS timeline (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, ts INTEGER NOT NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL, detail_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}')`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS monitor (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1), kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    ticks_left INTEGER NOT NULL, consecutive_passes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, schedule_id TEXT)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS postmortem (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1), markdown TEXT NOT NULL,
    generated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,
] as const;
import type { Category, Confidence, Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";
import type { CheckStatus, ToolName } from "../tools/types";

/** JSON values: anything stored in state or returned over RPC must be serialisable. */
export type Json = string | number | boolean | null | Json[] | { [key: string]: Json };
/** Timeline details are flat on purpose: a recursive type in the state makes RPC stub types too deep. */
export type Scalar = string | number | boolean | null;

/** Synced state (TDD §6.3): small on purpose; full evidence goes through `getEvidence`. */
export type IncidentStatus = "investigating" | "awaiting_approval" | "mitigated" | "resolved";
export type Origin = "chat" | "watch";
export type RunMode = "ai" | "rule_based";
export type RunKind = "initial" | "followup";
/** Why a run ended (TDD §8.2 "Limits keep progress"); `running` while it's active. */
export const RUN_STATUSES = ["running", "done", "limit_turns", "limit_tools", "limit_deadline", "limit_budget", "llm_unavailable", "failed"] as const;
export type RunStatus = (typeof RUN_STATUSES)[number];

export type CheckView = {
  evidenceId: string;
  runId: string;
  tool: ToolName;
  label: string;
  status: "pending" | "running" | CheckStatus;
  summary: string;
  startedAt: number;
  finishedAt?: number;
};
export type MessageView = { id: string; runId: string | null; role: "user" | "assistant" | "system"; content: string; createdAt: number };
export type ApprovalView = { id: string; runId: string; actionId: string; status: "pending" | "approved" | "rejected" | "expired" | "superseded"; createdAt: number; decidedAt?: number };
export type TimelineView = { id: number; ts: number; type: string; detail: Record<string, Scalar> };

export type IncidentState = {
  id: string;
  target: string;
  origin: Origin;
  status: IncidentStatus;
  category: Category | null;
  confidence: Confidence | null;
  /** Mode of the latest run. */
  mode: RunMode;
  /** Non-null → the composer is disabled (D13). */
  activeRunId: string | null;
  /** Latest run's findings (for the approval card's "why"). */
  findings: Findings | null;
  checks: CheckView[];
  messages: MessageView[];
  approvals: ApprovalView[];
  timeline: TimelineView[];
  monitor: { kind: "post_action" | "burst"; passes: number; ticksLeft: number } | null;
  hasPostmortem: boolean;
};

/** WebSocket side channel (broadcast, not state). `stream_start` resets the buffer when a retried narration restreams. */
export type StreamFrame = { type: "stream_start"; messageId: string } | { type: "delta"; messageId: string; text: string } | { type: "stream_end"; messageId: string };

export type ConnectionRole = "owner" | "viewer";
export type ConnectionInfo = { role: ConnectionRole };

export const STATE_CAPS = { checks: 40, messages: 50, approvals: 5, timeline: 100 } as const;

export const EMPTY_STATE: IncidentState = {
  id: "",
  target: "",
  origin: "chat",
  status: "investigating",
  category: null,
  confidence: null,
  mode: "ai",
  activeRunId: null,
  findings: null,
  checks: [],
  messages: [],
  approvals: [],
  timeline: [],
  monitor: null,
  hasPostmortem: false,
};
```

138	  /** One planning run at a time (D13). The check and insert run without an await in between. */

140	    if (!this.incident()) throw new Error("not_initialized");
141	    if (this.activeRunId()) throw new Error("run_active");

143	    const runId = `run-${seq}`;

145	    this.sql`INSERT INTO runs (id, seq, kind, question, status, started_at) VALUES (${runId}, ${seq}, ${kind}, ${question}, 'running', ${now})`;
146	    this.addMessage(runId, role, question);
147	    this.event("run_started", { runId, kind });
148	    this.touch();
149	    this.syncState();
150	    await this.upsertIndex();
151	    // Test seam: the integration tests drive some runs by hand (vitest.config.ts sets it); unset everywhere else.

229	  }

231	  async setFindings(runId: string, findings: Findings, mode: RunMode = "ai"): Promise<void> {
232	    const run = this.mustRun(runId);
233	    const incident = this.mustIncident();
234	    this.sql`UPDATE runs SET findings_json = ${JSON.stringify(findings)}, mode = ${mode} WHERE id = ${runId}`;
235	    this.sql`UPDATE incident SET category = ${findings.category}, confidence = ${findings.confidence}, mode = ${mode} WHERE id = ${incident.id}`;
236	    this.event("findings", { runId, category: findings.category, confidence: findings.confidence, mode });
237	    // A chat incident whose first check is healthy is resolved right away (TDD §10).

239	      await this.transition("resolved", "healthy on first check");

242	    this.touch();
243	    this.syncState();
244	    await this.upsertIndex();
245	  }

247	  /**
248	   * Streams the diagnosis (D4) and stores the final text. Idempotent per run. Never fails the run:
249	   * junk or missing output falls back to the rule template, and a well-formed answer that was cut short
250	   * is kept with a note (TDD §8.2 "Limits keep progress").
251	   */
252	  async narrate(runId: string): Promise<{ messageId: string; source: NarrationOutcome["source"] | "existing" }> {
253	    const messageId = `narr-${runId}`;
254	    if (this.sql`SELECT 1 FROM messages WHERE id = ${messageId}`.length) return { messageId, source: "existing" };
255	    const run = this.mustRun(runId);
256	    const findings = run.findings_json ? (JSON.parse(run.findings_json) as Findings) : null;
257	    const results = this.resultsFor(findings?.evidence ?? [], runId);
258	    const knownIds = this.sql<{ evidence_id: string }>`SELECT evidence_id FROM checks`.map((r) => r.evidence_id);
259	    const template = () => (findings ? renderTemplate(findings, results) : "No findings were recorded for this run.");
260	
261	    let outcome: NarrationOutcome;
262	    if (!findings || run.mode === "rule_based") {
263	      outcome = { text: template(), source: "template" };
264	    } else {
265	      this.frame({ type: "stream_start", messageId });
266	      try {
267	        const r = await streamLlm(
268	          llmDeps(this.env),
269	          { system: narrationSystem(), messages: narrationUser({ question: run.question, findings, evidence: results.map(toLine) }), maxTokens: NARRATION_MAX_TOKENS },
270	          (text) => this.frame({ type: "delta", messageId, text }),
271	        );
272	        outcome = finalizeNarration({ text: r.text, truncated: r.truncated, knownIds, template });
273	      } catch (e) {
274	        console.warn("narration failed", (e as Error).message);
275	        outcome = finalizeNarration({ partial: e instanceof LlmUnavailable ? e.partial : undefined, knownIds, template });
276	      }
277	      this.frame({ type: "stream_end", messageId });
278	    }
279	
280	    this.sql`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO messages (id, run_id, role, content, evidence_refs_json, created_at)
281	      VALUES (${messageId}, ${runId}, 'assistant', ${outcome.text}, ${JSON.stringify(findings?.evidence ?? [])}, ${Date.now()})`;
282	    this.event("narrated", { runId, source: outcome.source });
283	    this.sql`UPDATE incident SET unread = 1 WHERE id = ${this.name}`;
284	    this.touch();
285	    this.syncState();
286	    await this.upsertIndex();
287	    return { messageId, source: outcome.source };
288	  }
289	
290	  /** Ends a run and records why (TDD §8.2). Only the first call counts. */
291	  async finishRun(runId: string, status: Exclude<RunStatus, "running">, stats: { toolCalls?: number; llmTurns?: number } = {}): Promise<void> {
292	    const run = this.mustRun(runId);
293	    if (run.status !== "running") return;
294	    const now = Date.now();
295	    this.sql`UPDATE runs SET status = ${status}, finished_at = ${now}, tool_calls = ${stats.toolCalls ?? 0},
296	      llm_turns = ${stats.llmTurns ?? 0} WHERE id = ${runId}`;
297	    // Checks planned but never run (the run stopped first) shouldn't spin forever in the UI.
298	    this.sql`UPDATE checks SET status = 'unavailable', summary = 'Not run: the run stopped before this check.', finished_at = ${now}
299	      WHERE run_id = ${runId} AND finished_at IS NULL`;
300	    this.event("run_finished", { runId, status });
301	    this.touch();
302	    this.syncState();
303	    await this.upsertIndex();
304	  }

347	  async transition(to: IncidentStatus, reason: string): Promise<void> {
348	    const from = this.mustIncident().status;

358	  private incident(): IncidentRow | undefined {
359	    try {
360	      return this.sql<IncidentRow>`SELECT * FROM incident LIMIT 1`[0];

372	  private mustRun(runId: string): RunRow {

374	    if (!run) throw new Error(`unknown run ${runId}`);

382	  /** Checks cited by the findings plus this run's finished checks, as ToolResults for the template/prompt. */
383	  private resultsFor(ids: string[], runId: string): ToolResult[] {
384	    const rows = this.sql<CheckRow>`SELECT * FROM checks WHERE finished_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY rowid`;
385	    const wanted = new Set(ids);
386	    return rows.filter((r) => wanted.has(r.evidence_id) || r.run_id === runId).map(toResult);

389	  private addMessage(runId: string | null, role: "user" | "assistant" | "system", content: string) {

391	  }
392	
393	  private event(type: string, detail: Record<string, Scalar>) {
394	    this.sql`INSERT INTO timeline (ts, event_type, detail_json) VALUES (${Date.now()}, ${type}, ${JSON.stringify(detail)})`;
395	  }
396	
397	  private touch() {
398	    this.sql`UPDATE incident SET updated_at = ${Date.now()} WHERE id = ${this.name}`;
399	  }
400	
401	  private frame(f: StreamFrame) {
402	    this.broadcast(JSON.stringify(f));
403	  }
404	
405	  /** Rebuilds the synced state from SQL after every change, so it can't drift. */
406	  private syncState() {
407	    const i = this.incident();
408	    if (!i) return;
409	    const latest = this.sql<{ findings_json: string }>`SELECT findings_json FROM runs WHERE findings_json IS NOT NULL ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1`[0];
410	    const checks = this.sql<CheckRow>`SELECT * FROM checks ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT ${STATE_CAPS.checks}`.reverse();
411	    const messages = this.sql<{ id: string; run_id: string | null; role: "user" | "assistant" | "system"; content: string; created_at: number }>`
412	      SELECT id, run_id, role, content, created_at FROM messages ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT ${STATE_CAPS.messages}`.reverse();
413	    const approvals = this.sql<{ id: string; run_id: string; action_id: string; status: IncidentState["approvals"][number]["status"]; created_at: number; decided_at: number | null }>`
414	      SELECT id, run_id, action_id, status, created_at, decided_at FROM approvals ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ${STATE_CAPS.approvals}`;
415	    const timeline = this.sql<{ id: number; ts: number; event_type: string; detail_json: string }>`
416	      SELECT * FROM timeline ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ${STATE_CAPS.timeline}`.reverse();
417	    const monitor = this.sql<{ kind: "post_action" | "burst"; ticks_left: number; consecutive_passes: number }>`SELECT * FROM monitor WHERE id = 1`[0];
418	
419	    this.setState({
420	      id: i.id,
421	      target: i.target,
422	      origin: i.origin,
423	      status: i.status,
424	      category: (i.category as IncidentState["category"]) ?? null,

438	      })),
439	      messages: messages.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, runId: m.run_id, role: m.role, content: m.content, createdAt: m.created_at })),
440	      approvals: approvals.map((a) => ({ id: a.id, runId: a.run_id, actionId: a.action_id, status: a.status, createdAt: a.created_at, ...(a.decided_at ? { decidedAt: a.decided_at } : {}) })),
441	      timeline: timeline.map((t) => ({ id: t.id, ts: t.ts, type: t.event_type, detail: JSON.parse(t.detail_json) as Record<string, Scalar> })),
442	      monitor: monitor ? { kind: monitor.kind, passes: monitor.consecutive_passes, ticksLeft: monitor.ticks_left } : null,
443	      hasPostmortem: this.sql`SELECT 1 FROM postmortem WHERE id = 1`.length > 0,
444	    });
445	  }
446	
447	  /** Mirrors the incident into D1 for the sidebar (D16). Errors are logged: `onStart` reconciles. */
448	  private async upsertIndex() {
449	    const i = this.incident();
450	    if (!i) return;
451	    try {
452	      await this.env.DB.prepare(
453	        `INSERT INTO incidents_index (id, workspace_id, target, status, origin, unread, created_at, updated_at)
454	         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)
455	         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET status = excluded.status, unread = excluded.unread, updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
456	      )
457	        .bind(i.id, i.workspace_id, i.target, i.status, i.origin, i.unread, i.created_at, i.updated_at)
458	        .run();
459	    } catch (e) {
460	      console.error("incidents_index upsert failed", (e as Error).message);
461	    }
462	  }
463	}
464	
465	/**
466	 * WebSocket callables. `@callable()` only records the method in the SDK's registry (its decorator context
467	 * is unused), so registering by hand avoids a Babel decorator transform in Vite and Vitest.
468	 */
469	for (const name of ["sendMessage", "resolve", "markRead", "getEvidence", "whoami"] as const) {
470	  callable()(IncidentAgent.prototype[name] as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown, undefined as unknown as ClassMethodDecoratorContext);
471	}
472	
473	function toResult(r: CheckRow): ToolResult {
474	  const status = r.status as ToolResult["status"];
475	  return {
476	    evidenceId: r.evidence_id,
477	    tool: r.tool,
478	    ok: status === "pass" || status === "fail",
479	    status,
480	    summary: r.summary,
481	    digest: r.digest ?? "{}",
482	    raw: r.raw_json ? JSON.parse(r.raw_json) : null,
483	  };
484	}
485	
486	function toLine(r: CheckRow | ToolResult): EvidenceLine {
487	  return "evidence_id" in r
488	    ? { evidenceId: r.evidence_id, tool: r.tool, status: r.status as EvidenceLine["status"], summary: r.summary, digest: r.digest ?? "{}" }
489	    : { evidenceId: r.evidenceId, tool: r.tool, status: r.status, summary: r.summary, digest: r.digest };

13	/** Bounds for one run (TDD §5.2). `tailReserve` keeps room for rules, findings, narrate and finish. */
14	export type DiagnoseLimits = { maxTurns: number; maxTools: number; deadlineMs: number; budget: number; tailReserve: number };

23	export const PLAN_MAX_TOKENS = 1500;
24	/** An LLM turn costs up to 2 subrequests (primary + fallback) plus 1 for `addPendingChecks`. */
25	const TURN_COST = 3;
26	/** The cheapest check: `dns_lookup` for A + AAAA on two resolvers. Below this, another tool can't fit. */
27	const CHEAPEST_TOOL = 4;
28	/** Planning-turn index used for the rule path's own checks, so its evidence IDs are retry-safe too. */
29	const RULES_TURN = 99;

31	/** The agent methods a run calls (TDD §7.2). */
32	export type RunAgent = {
33	  getRunContext(runId: string): Promise<RunContext>;
34	  addPendingChecks(runId: string, turn: number, calls: { tool: ToolName; args: unknown }[]): Promise<string[]>;
35	  recordCheck(evidenceId: string, result: ToolResult): Promise<void>;
36	  ruleFindings(runId: string, modelArgs: unknown): Promise<Findings>;
37	  setFindings(runId: string, findings: Findings, mode: RunMode): Promise<void>;
38	  narrate(runId: string): Promise<unknown>;
39	  finishRun(runId: string, status: Exclude<RunStatus, "running">, stats: { toolCalls: number; llmTurns: number }): Promise<void>;
40	};
41	
42	/**
43	 * What the Workflow provides. `step` is `step.do` (durable, retried; results replay after a restart);
44	 * `now` is only called inside steps, so every time-based decision replays the same way.
45	 */
46	export type RunIO = {
47	  step<T>(name: string, fn: () => Promise<T>, opts?: { retries?: number }): Promise<T>;
48	  now(): number;
49	  agent: RunAgent;
50	  llm(req: LlmRequest): Promise<LlmResult>;
51	  tool(name: ToolName, args: unknown, evidenceId: string, scope: Scope): Promise<{ result: ToolResult; subrequests: number }>;
52	};
53	
54	export type ValidCall = { tool: ToolName; args: unknown; cap: number };
55	export type Validated = { tools: ValidCall[]; findings: Findings | null; findingsArgs: unknown; invalid: string[] };
56	
57	/**
58	 * Validates one turn's calls (FR-12): zod via `prepare`, the origin pin (D6), JSON that didn't parse,
59	 * and `submit_findings` (whose evidence must cite checks that exist).
60	 */
61	export function validateCalls(calls: ToolCall[], scope: Scope, knownIds: readonly string[]): Validated {
62	  const out: Validated = { tools: [], findings: null, findingsArgs: null, invalid: [] };
63	  const known = new Set(knownIds);
64	  for (const call of calls) {
65	    if (call.argsError) {
66	      out.invalid.push(`${call.name}: ${call.argsError}`);
67	      continue;
68	    }
69	    if (call.name === SUBMIT_FINDINGS) {
70	      out.findingsArgs = call.args;
71	      const parsed = parseFindings(call.args);
72	      if (!parsed.ok) {
73	        out.invalid.push(`submit_findings: ${parsed.error}`);
74	        continue;
75	      }
76	      const evidence = parsed.findings.evidence.filter((id) => known.has(id));
77	      if (evidence.length === 0) {
78	        out.invalid.push(`submit_findings: evidence must cite checks from this incident (${[...known].join(", ") || "none yet"}).`);
79	        continue;
80	      }
81	      out.findings = { ...parsed.findings, evidence };
82	      continue;
83	    }
84	    const tool = TOOLS[call.name as ToolName];
85	    if (!tool) {
86	      out.invalid.push(`Unknown tool ${call.name}.`);
87	      continue;
88	    }
89	    const prepared = tool.prepare(call.args);
90	    if (!prepared.ok) {
91	      out.invalid.push(`Invalid arguments for ${call.name}: ${prepared.error}`);
92	      continue;
93	    }
94	    try {
95	      assertInScope(call.name, prepared.args, scope);
96	    } catch (e) {
97	      if (!(e instanceof OutOfScopeError)) throw e;
98	      out.invalid.push(e.message);
99	      continue;
100	    }
101	    out.tools.push({ tool: tool.name, args: prepared.args, cap: prepared.cap });
102	  }
103	  return out;
104	}
105	
106	type TurnResult =
107	  | { kind: "unavailable"; reason: string; attempts: number; at: number }
108	  | ({ kind: "turn"; attempts: number; at: number } & Validated);
109	
110	type CheckOut = { line: EvidenceLine; tool: ToolName; cnameTargets: string[]; subrequests: number; at: number };
111	
112	export type RunSummary = { status: Exclude<RunStatus, "running">; mode: RunMode; toolCalls: number; llmTurns: number; category?: string };
113	
114	/**
115	 * One bounded diagnosis run (TDD §8.2). Every limit ends in findings built from what was gathered
116	 * ("Limits keep progress"): the turn and tool caps end with a finish-only turn; the deadline, the LLM being
117	 * unavailable or a second invalid call go to the rules, which keep the model's valid fields.
118	 */
119	export async function diagnose(io: RunIO, runId: string, limits: DiagnoseLimits = DEFAULT_LIMITS): Promise<RunSummary> {
120	  const stats = { toolCalls: 0, llmTurns: 0 };
121	  let mode: RunMode = "ai";
122	  try {
123	    const ctx = await io.step("context", async () => ({ ...(await io.agent.getRunContext(runId)), startedAt: io.now() }));
124	    const budget = new SubrequestBudget(limits.budget);
125	    budget.settle(0, 1);
126	    budget.reserve(limits.tailReserve);
127	
128	    const deadline = ctx.startedAt + limits.deadlineMs;
129	    let lastAt = ctx.startedAt;
130	    const evidence: EvidenceLine[] = [...ctx.evidence];
131	    // CNAME targets found during the run widen the origin pin for dns_lookup (D6).
132	    const cnameTargets = [...ctx.scope.cnameTargets];
133	    const scope: Scope = { host: ctx.scope.host, cnameTargets };
134	    const ranThisRun = new Set<ToolName>();
135	    mode = ctx.mode;
136	
137	    let findings: Findings | null = null;
138	    let lastFindingsArgs: unknown = null;
139	    let limit: Exclude<RunStatus, "running" | "done" | "failed"> | null = null;
140	    let invalidTurns = 0;
141	    let feedback: string | undefined;
142	
143	    /** Runs one check in its own step. Never throws: a broken check is stored as an `error` result. */
144	    const runCheck = async (id: string, call: ValidCall): Promise<void> => {
145	      if (!budget.reserve(call.cap)) {
146	        limit ??= "limit_budget";
147	        return;
148	      }
149	      let out: CheckOut;
150	      try {
151	        out = await io.step(`tool-${id}`, async () => {
152	          let result: ToolResult;
153	          let subrequests = 0;
154	          try {
155	            ({ result, subrequests } = await io.tool(call.tool, call.args, id, scope));
156	          } catch (e) {
157	            result = errorResult(id, call.tool, e);
158	          }
159	          await io.agent.recordCheck(id, result);
160	          const cnameTargets = result.tool === "dns_lookup" && result.ok ? ((result.raw as DnsRaw).cnameTargets ?? []) : [];
161	          return { line: toLine(result), tool: call.tool, cnameTargets, subrequests, at: io.now() };
162	        });
163	      } catch {
164	        // The step failed even after retries (e.g. the agent RPC): count the worst case and move on.
165	        out = { line: toLine(errorResult(id, call.tool, new Error("check could not be recorded"))), tool: call.tool, cnameTargets: [], subrequests: call.cap, at: lastAt };
166	      }
167	      budget.settle(call.cap, out.subrequests + 1);
168	      stats.toolCalls++;
169	      lastAt = out.at;
170	      evidence.push(out.line);
171	      ranThisRun.add(out.tool);
172	      for (const t of out.cnameTargets) if (!cnameTargets.includes(t)) cnameTargets.push(t);
173	    };
174	
175	    if (mode === "ai") {
176	      for (let turn = 1; turn <= limits.maxTurns; turn++) {
177	        if (lastAt >= deadline) {
178	          limit ??= "limit_deadline";
179	          break;
180	        }
181	        if (budget.remaining() < TURN_COST) {
182	          limit ??= "limit_budget";
183	          break;
184	        }
185	        const toolsLeft = limits.maxTools - stats.toolCalls;
186	        const finishReason =
187	          turn === limits.maxTurns ? "limit_turns" : toolsLeft <= 0 ? "limit_tools" : budget.remaining() < TURN_COST + CHEAPEST_TOOL ? "limit_budget" : null;
188	        const knownIds = evidence.map((e) => e.evidenceId);
189	        const r = await io.step(`llm-turn-${turn}`, async (): Promise<TurnResult> => {
190	          try {
191	            const res = await io.llm({
192	              system: planningSystem({ target: ctx.target, toolsLeft: finishReason ? 0 : toolsLeft, turnsLeft: limits.maxTurns - turn + 1 }),
193	              messages: planningUser({ question: ctx.question, evidence, ...(feedback ? { invalid: feedback } : {}) }),
194	              tools: llmTools({ finishOnly: !!finishReason }),
195	              toolChoice: "required",
196	              maxTokens: PLAN_MAX_TOKENS,
197	            });
198	            const v = validateCalls(res.toolCalls, scope, knownIds);
199	            if (res.toolCalls.length === 0) v.invalid.push("No tool call. Always call a tool or submit_findings.");
200	            return { kind: "turn", ...v, attempts: res.fallback ? 2 : 1, at: io.now() };
201	          } catch (e) {
202	            if (e instanceof LlmUnavailable) return { kind: "unavailable", reason: e.message, attempts: 2, at: io.now() };
203	            throw e;
204	          }
205	        });
206	        budget.settle(0, r.attempts);
207	        stats.llmTurns++;
208	        lastAt = r.at;
209	
210	        if (r.kind === "unavailable") {
211	          mode = "rule_based";
212	          break;
213	        }
214	        if (r.findingsArgs !== null) lastFindingsArgs = r.findingsArgs;
215	        if (r.findings) {
216	          findings = r.findings;
217	          if (finishReason) limit ??= finishReason;
218	          break;
219	        }
220	        if (finishReason) {
221	          // The finish-only turn didn't produce usable findings: the rules fill in, keeping its valid fields.
222	          limit ??= finishReason;
223	          break;
224	        }
225	        if (r.invalid.length && ++invalidTurns >= 2) break;
226	
227	        const notes = [...r.invalid];
228	        const calls = r.tools.slice(0, toolsLeft);
229	        if (r.tools.length > calls.length) notes.push(`Only ${toolsLeft} tool call(s) were left, so ${r.tools.length - calls.length} call(s) were dropped.`);
230	        feedback = notes.length ? notes.join("\n") : undefined;
231	        if (calls.length === 0) continue;
232	
233	        const ids = await io.step(`pending-${turn}`, () => io.agent.addPendingChecks(runId, turn, calls.map(({ tool, args }) => ({ tool, args }))));
234	        budget.settle(0, 1);
235	        for (const [i, call] of calls.entries()) {
236	          if (lastAt >= deadline) {
237	            limit ??= "limit_deadline";
238	            break;
239	          }
240	          await runCheck(ids[i]!, call);
241	        }
242	        if (limit === "limit_deadline") break;
243	      }
244	    }
245	
246	    if (!findings) {
247	      // Rule path / gap fill (TDD §8.2): the baseline checks this run hasn't done, if budget and time allow.
248	      const baseline = (
249	        [
250	          ["dns_lookup", { hostname: scope.host }],
251	          ["http_probe", { url: ctx.target }],
252	        ] as const
253	      ).filter(([tool]) => !ranThisRun.has(tool));
254	      const calls = baseline.flatMap(([tool, args]) => {
255	        const prepared = TOOLS[tool]?.prepare(args);
256	        return prepared?.ok ? [{ tool, args: prepared.args, cap: prepared.cap }] : [];
257	      });
258	      if (calls.length && lastAt < deadline && budget.remaining() >= 1 + Math.min(...calls.map((c) => c.cap))) {
259	        const ids = await io.step("pending-rules", () => io.agent.addPendingChecks(runId, RULES_TURN, calls.map(({ tool, args }) => ({ tool, args }))));
260	        budget.settle(0, 1);
261	        for (const [i, call] of calls.entries()) {
262	          if (lastAt >= deadline) break;
263	          await runCheck(ids[i]!, call);
264	        }
265	      }
266	      const args = lastFindingsArgs;
267	      findings = await io.step("rules", () => io.agent.ruleFindings(runId, args));
268	    }
269	
270	    const final = findings;
271	    await io.step("findings", () => io.agent.setFindings(runId, final, mode));
272	    await io.step("narrate", () => io.agent.narrate(runId));
273	    // WP-12: propose an action and wait for approval here.
274	    const status = limit ?? (mode === "rule_based" && ctx.mode === "ai" ? "llm_unavailable" : "done");
275	    await io.step("finish", () => io.agent.finishRun(runId, status, stats));
276	    return { status, mode, ...stats, category: final.category };
277	  } catch (e) {
278	    console.error("diagnosis run failed", runId, (e as Error).message);
279	    try {
280	      await io.step("finish-failed", () => io.agent.finishRun(runId, "failed", stats));
281	    } catch {
282	      // Nothing more to do: the agent's activeRunId stays set until someone resolves the incident.
283	    }
284	    return { status: "failed", mode, ...stats };
285	  }
286	}
287	
288	function errorResult(evidenceId: string, tool: ToolName, e: unknown): ToolResult {
289	  const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
290	  return { evidenceId, tool, ok: false, status: "error", summary: `Check failed: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`, digest: JSON.stringify({ error: message.slice(0, 200) }), raw: null };
291	}
292	
293	function toLine(r: ToolResult): EvidenceLine {
294	  return { evidenceId: r.evidenceId, tool: r.tool, status: r.status, summary: r.summary, digest: r.digest };
295	}

import type { Agent } from "agents";
import { AgentWorkflow, type AgentWorkflowEvent, type AgentWorkflowStep } from "agents/workflows";
import type { IncidentAgent } from "../agent/IncidentAgent";
import { callLlm, llmDeps } from "../llm/client";
import { diagnose, type RunIO, type RunSummary } from "../loop/plan";
import { devAllowOrigin } from "../safety/ssrf";
import { runTool } from "../tools/registry";

export type DiagnosisParams = { incidentId: string; runId: string };

/**
 * One bounded diagnosis run (D3, TDD §8.2). All decisions live in `loop/plan.ts`; this class only maps
 * them onto durable steps, the incident's agent and the bindings. Steps retry with backoff; tool steps
 * spend subrequest budget on every retry, so they get the same small limit.
 */
export class DiagnosisWorkflow extends AgentWorkflow<Agent, DiagnosisParams> {
  override async run(event: AgentWorkflowEvent<DiagnosisParams>, step: AgentWorkflowStep): Promise<RunSummary> {
    // `AgentWorkflow` only types agents with `unknown` state, so the stub is narrowed here once.
    const agent = this.agent as unknown as DurableObjectStub<IncidentAgent>;
    const env = this.env;
    const deps = llmDeps(env);
    const allowOrigin = devAllowOrigin(env);

    const io: RunIO = {
      step: <T>(name: string, fn: () => Promise<T>, opts?: { retries?: number }) =>
        step.do(name, { retries: { limit: opts?.retries ?? 2, delay: "1 second", backoff: "exponential" } }, fn as () => Promise<never>) as Promise<T>,
      now: () => Date.now(),
      agent: {
        getRunContext: (runId) => agent.getRunContext(runId),
        addPendingChecks: (runId, turn, calls) => agent.addPendingChecks(runId, turn, calls),
        recordCheck: (id, result) => agent.recordCheck(id, result),
        ruleFindings: (runId, args) => agent.ruleFindings(runId, args),
        setFindings: (runId, findings, mode) => agent.setFindings(runId, findings, mode),
        narrate: (runId) => agent.narrate(runId),
        finishRun: (runId, status, stats) => agent.finishRun(runId, status, stats),
      },
      llm: (req) => callLlm(deps, req),
      tool: async (name, args, evidenceId, scope) => {
        const out = await runTool(name, args, { fetch: (input, init) => fetch(input, init), evidenceId, ...(allowOrigin ? { allowOrigin } : {}) }, scope);
        if (!out.ok) throw new Error(out.invalid);
        return { result: out.result, subrequests: out.subrequests };
      },
    };
    return diagnose(io, event.payload.runId);
  }
}
import type { Fetch } from "../lib/doh";
import { countingFetch, HIGH_LATENCY_TOTAL_MS, SubrequestCounter } from "../loop/budget";
import { followHops, type HopStop } from "../safety/reader";
import { createGuard } from "../safety/ssrf";

/** Guard DoH (A + AAAA) + 1 GET following up to 5 hops = 8; 10 leaves room for one cross-host redirect. */
export const QUICKCHECK_CAP = 10;

export type QuickCheckResult = {
  pass: boolean;
  status: number | null;
  totalMs: number;
  stop: HopStop | "budget";
  /** Why it didn't pass: an SSRF code, "timeout"/"network", "http_<status>", "slow", "loop", … */
  reason?: string;
  subrequests: number;
};

/** One cheap health check with no LLM (cron, monitor ticks, verify after an action). */
export async function quickCheck(
  url: string,
  deps: { fetch: Fetch; allowOrigin?: string; now?: () => number; cap?: number },
): Promise<QuickCheckResult> {
  const counter = new SubrequestCounter(deps.cap ?? QUICKCHECK_CAP);
  const fetchFn = countingFetch(deps.fetch, counter);
  const guard = createGuard({ fetch: fetchFn, allowOrigin: deps.allowOrigin });

  let r;
  try {
    r = await followHops(fetchFn, guard, url, "GET", { now: deps.now });
  } catch (e) {
    if ((e as Error).name === "BudgetExceededError") {
      return { pass: false, status: null, totalMs: 0, stop: "budget", reason: "budget", subrequests: counter.count };
    }
    throw e;
  }

  const totalMs = r.hops.reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.totalMs, 0);
  const status = r.stop === "done" ? (r.final?.status ?? null) : null;
  let reason: string | undefined;
  if (r.stop === "blocked") reason = r.blocked?.code;
  else if (r.stop === "error") reason = r.error?.error;
  else if (r.stop !== "done") reason = r.stop;
  else if (status === null || status >= 400) reason = `http_${status}`;
  else if (totalMs >= HIGH_LATENCY_TOTAL_MS) reason = "slow";

  return { pass: reason === undefined, status, totalMs, stop: r.stop, ...(reason ? { reason } : {}), subrequests: counter.count };
}
```

export function IncidentView({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const { state, feed, role, notFound, send, getEvidence } = useIncident(id);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const knownIds = useMemo(() => new Set(state?.checks.map((c) => c.evidenceId) ?? []), [state?.checks]);
  const host = state ? new URL(state.target).hostname : "";
  const other = state ? otherHost(text, host) : null;
  const status = state?.status;

  // Keep the sidebar's badge in step with the open incident.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status) notifyIncidentsChanged();
  }, [status]);

  if (notFound) {
    return <p className="m-auto p-8 text-(--cf-fg-muted)">This incident doesn't exist.</p>;
  }
  if (!state) {
    return (
      <div className="m-auto flex items-center gap-2 p-8 text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
        <Loader size={16} /> Connecting…
      </div>
    );
  }

  const viewer = role === "viewer";
  const note = runNote(state.timeline);

  const onSend = async (message: string) => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await send(message);
      setText("");
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message.includes("run_active") ? "Still working on the last question. Try again when it finishes." : "Couldn't send that. Try again.");
    }
  };

  const newIncidentFor = async () => {
    const r = await createIncident(text);
    if (!r.ok) return setError(ERROR_TEXT[r.error]);
    setText("");
    notifyIncidentsChanged();
    navigate(`/i/${r.id}`);
  };

  return (
    // shrink-0: as a flex child of the scrolling <main> it would otherwise shrink to one screen, and the
    // sticky header and composer would scroll away with it.

      <div className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-(--cf-border) bg-(--cf-bg-100) px-4 py-3">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-3xl flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <h1 className="min-w-0 truncate font-mono text-base">{state.target}</h1>
          <IncidentStatusBadge status={state.status} />
          {state.category && <span className="text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)">· {CATEGORY_TEXT[state.category] ?? state.category}</span>}
          {viewer && (
            <Badge variant="neutral" icon={<EyeIcon aria-hidden size={14} />} className="ml-auto">
              View only
            </Badge>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto mt-3 flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-2 px-4">
        {viewer && <ReadOnlyBanner />}
        {state.mode === "rule_based" && (
          <Banner variant="alert" icon={<RobotIcon aria-hidden />} title="AI unavailable, rule-based diagnosis" description="The checks ran and fixed rules picked the diagnosis." />
        )}
        {note && !state.activeRunId && <Banner variant="secondary" icon={<InfoIcon aria-hidden />} description={note} />}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1">
        <ChatPane feed={feed} knownIds={knownIds} getEvidence={getEvidence} />
      </div>

      {!viewer && (
        <div className="sticky bottom-0 z-30 bg-(--cf-bg-100) pt-2">
          <Composer
            value={text}
            onChange={setText}
            onSend={onSend}
            disabled={!!state.activeRunId || role === null}
            disabledReason={state.activeRunId ? "Working on it… you can ask a follow-up when this run finishes." : undefined}
            placeholder="Ask a follow-up, e.g. is it still failing?"
            notice={
              other ? (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-(--cf-fg-muted)">
                  <span>
                    That's a different host, and checks stay on <span className="font-mono">{host}</span>.
                  </span>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => void newIncidentFor()}>
                    New incident for <span className="font-mono">{other}</span>
                  </Button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>
              )
            }
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ReadOnlyBanner() {
  return (
    <Banner
      variant="secondary"
      icon={<EyeIcon aria-hidden />}
      title="View only"
      description="You opened a shared link. You can follow this incident and open its evidence, but only its owner can ask questions or take actions."

  );

import { useAgent } from "agents/react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { ConnectionRole, IncidentState, Json } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { buildFeed } from "../lib/feed";
import { applyFrame, parseFrame, type Streams } from "../lib/stream";

export type Evidence = { evidenceId: string; tool: string; status: string; summary: string; raw: Json };

/**
 * One incident over the agent's WebSocket (TDD §12.3): synced state, the live narration buffer, the
 * caller's role, and the callables. The state comes only from the server (client writes are rejected).
 */
export function useIncident(id: string) {
  const [streams, setStreams] = useState<Streams>({});
  const [role, setRole] = useState<ConnectionRole | null>(null);
  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false);

  const agent = useAgent<IncidentState>({
    agent: "incident-agent",
    name: id,
    onMessage: (e: MessageEvent) => {
      const frame = parseFrame(e.data);
      if (frame) setStreams((s) => applyFrame(s, frame));
    },
    onClose: (e: CloseEvent) => {
      // 4404: the agent has no such incident (closed in onConnect).
      if (e?.code === 4404) setNotFound(true);
    },
  });

  const state = agent.state?.id ? agent.state : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state) return;
    let live = true;
    agent
      .call("whoami")
      .then((r) => live && setRole((r as { role: ConnectionRole }).role))
      .catch(() => live && setRole("viewer"));
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
    // Ask once the first state arrives; the role doesn't change for a connection.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [!!state]);

  const feed = useMemo(() => (state ? buildFeed(state, streams) : []), [state, streams]);

  const send = useCallback(async (text: string) => (await agent.call("sendMessage", [text])) as { runId: string }, [agent]);
  const getEvidence = useCallback(async (evidenceId: string) => (await agent.call("getEvidence", [evidenceId])) as Evidence | null, [agent]);

  return { state, feed, role, notFound, send, getEvidence };
}
import { cn } from "@cloudflare/kumo";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { MessageView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import type { Evidence } from "../hooks/useIncident";
import type { FeedItem } from "../lib/feed";
import { CheckCard } from "./CheckCard";
import { Markdown } from "./Markdown";

type Props = { feed: FeedItem[]; knownIds: ReadonlySet<string>; getEvidence: (id: string) => Promise<Evidence | null> };

/** The incident conversation: messages, check cards in run order, and the live narration (TDD §12.2). */
export function ChatPane({ feed, knownIds, getEvidence }: Props) {
  const end = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const last = feed.at(-1);
  const lastKey = last ? `${last.key}:${last.kind === "stream" ? last.text.length : ""}` : "";
  useEffect(() => {
    end.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: "end" });
  }, [lastKey]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-3 px-4 py-6">
      {feed.map((item) => {
        switch (item.kind) {
          case "message":
            return <MessageBubble key={item.key} message={item.message} knownIds={knownIds} />;
          case "check":
            return <CheckCard key={item.key} check={item.check} status={item.status} getEvidence={getEvidence} />;
          case "stream":
            return (
              <div key={item.key} aria-live="polite" aria-busy="true" className="rounded-lg bg-(--cf-bg-200) p-4">
                <Markdown text={item.text || "…"} knownIds={knownIds} />
              </div>
            );
        }
      })}
      <div ref={end} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function MessageBubble({ message, knownIds }: { message: MessageView; knownIds: ReadonlySet<string> }) {
  if (message.role === "user") {
    return (
      <div className="ml-auto max-w-[85%] rounded-lg bg-(--cf-bg-300) px-4 py-2 whitespace-pre-wrap">
        <span className="sr-only">You: </span>
        {message.content}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "rounded-lg p-4",
        message.role === "system" ? "border border-dashed border-(--cf-border) text-sm text-(--cf-fg-muted)" : "bg-(--cf-bg-200)",
      )}
    >
      <Markdown text={message.content} knownIds={knownIds} />
    </div>
  );
}
import type { IncidentStatus, RunStatus, TimelineView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { parseTarget } from "../../worker/safety/target";

/**
 * A follow-up that names a different host than the incident's (TDD §8.6). Tools are pinned to the incident's
 * origin (D6), so the UI offers a new incident instead of sending. "demo" alone doesn't count.
 */
export function otherHost(text: string, incidentHost: string): string | null {
  const r = parseTarget(text, "https://demo.invalid/");
  if (!r.ok || r.source === "demo") return null;
  return r.host === incidentHost.toLowerCase() ? null : r.host;
}

const LIMIT_NOTES: Partial<Record<RunStatus, string>> = {
  limit_turns: "Stopped at the 3-turn limit.",
  limit_tools: "Stopped at the 6-check limit.",
  limit_budget: "Stopped when this run's request budget ran out.",
  limit_deadline: "Stopped at the 60-second limit.",
};

/**
 * Says which limit ended the latest run (TDD §8.2 "Limits keep progress"), or that it failed. Nothing while
 * a newer run is in progress, or when the run simply finished.
 */
export function runNote(timeline: readonly TimelineView[]): string | null {
  const last = [...timeline].reverse().find((t) => t.type === "run_finished" || t.type === "run_started");
  if (!last || last.type !== "run_finished") return null;
  const status = last.detail.status as RunStatus;
  if (status === "failed") return "This run failed before it finished. Try asking again.";
  const note = LIMIT_NOTES[status];
  return note ? `${note} The diagnosis uses the checks it completed.` : null;
}

export const STATUS_TEXT: Record<IncidentStatus, string> = {
  investigating: "Investigating",
  awaiting_approval: "Awaiting approval",
  mitigated: "Mitigated",
  resolved: "Resolved",
};

export const CATEGORY_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {
  dns_failure: "DNS failure",
  dns_mismatch: "DNS mismatch",
  tls_expiry: "TLS / certificate",
  origin_5xx: "Origin 5xx",
  edge_error: "Edge error",
  high_latency: "High latency",
  redirect_loop: "Redirect loop",
  cache_misconfiguration: "Cache misconfiguration",
  provider_outage: "Provider outage",
  healthy: "Healthy",
  inconclusive: "Inconclusive",
};

import type { CheckView, IncidentState, MessageView } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import type { Streams } from "./stream";

export type CheckDisplayStatus = CheckView["status"];

export type FeedItem =
  | { kind: "message"; key: string; message: MessageView }
  | { kind: "check"; key: string; check: CheckView; status: CheckDisplayStatus }
  | { kind: "stream"; key: string; messageId: string; text: string };

/**
 * The chat in run order: each run's opening message, then that run's checks, then its narration. While a
 * narration is still streaming, its live buffer takes the narration's place. Messages without a run (and
 * checks of runs without an opening message) keep their time order.
 */
export function buildFeed(state: Pick<IncidentState, "messages" | "checks" | "activeRunId">, streams: Streams): FeedItem[] {
  const stored = new Set(state.messages.map((m) => m.id));
  const checksByRun = new Map<string, CheckView[]>();
  for (const c of state.checks) checksByRun.set(c.runId, [...(checksByRun.get(c.runId) ?? []), c]);

  const items: FeedItem[] = [];
  const opened = new Set<string>();
  const openRun = (runId: string) => {
    opened.add(runId);
    for (const c of checksByRun.get(runId) ?? []) {
      // Pending checks of the active run are being worked on right now.
      const status = c.status === "pending" && state.activeRunId === runId ? "running" : c.status;
      items.push({ kind: "check", key: `check-${c.evidenceId}`, check: c, status });
    }
    const stream = streams[`narr-${runId}`];
    if (stream && !stored.has(stream.messageId)) items.push({ kind: "stream", key: `stream-${stream.messageId}`, messageId: stream.messageId, text: stream.text });
  };

  for (const m of state.messages) {
    items.push({ kind: "message", key: `msg-${m.id}`, message: m });
    if (m.runId && m.role !== "assistant" && !opened.has(m.runId)) openRun(m.runId);
  }
  for (const runId of checksByRun.keys()) if (!opened.has(runId)) openRun(runId);
  return items;
}

| Request | `{messages, tools, max_tokens, tool_choice?, stream?}`. Tools use the OpenAI shape `{type:"function", function:{name, description, parameters}}`. A tool result goes back as `{role:"tool", tool_call_id, content}` after the assistant message that holds `tool_calls`. | One mapper for both models. gpt-oss-120b also accepts the Responses format, but it rejected these tool definitions and offers nothing extra, so it isn't used. |
| Response | `choices[0].message.{content, tool_calls[], reasoning, reasoning_content}` and `finish_reason` (`tool_calls` or `stop`). `tool_calls[i] = {id, type:"function", function:{name, arguments}}`, where **`arguments` is a JSON string**. | `JSON.parse` the arguments, then validate with zod. A parse failure counts as an invalid call (FR-12). `reasoning*` is **never** shown, stored or sent back to the model. |
| `submit_findings` | With the default `tool_choice`, gpt answered in prose after the tool result and **didn't** call `submit_findings`. glm called it, but put whole sentences in `evidence[]`. `tool_choice:"required"` made gpt call it. Naming the tool in `tool_choice` was ignored. | Planning turns send **`tool_choice:"required"`**. The schema says `evidence` holds IDs only (`dns-1`); zod accepts `[dns-1]` or `dns-1` and rejects anything else. Prose `content` on a tool turn is ignored. |
| glm thinking | On by default. It took 12–21 s, and once returned `content: null` because reasoning used the whole `max_tokens`. With `chat_template_kwargs:{enable_thinking:false}` it takes about 2 s. | glm is **always** called with thinking off. |
| gpt reasoning | Can't be turned off; `reasoning_effort` low/medium/high (default medium). Text took about 3 s, a tool turn 1.4–1.7 s, but the forced `submit_findings` turn took **19 s** once. | Watch latency against `RUN_DEADLINE_MS` in WP-09. Use `reasoning_effort:"low"` if turns run slow. |
| Streaming | `stream:true` returns an SSE `ReadableStream`, and it works through the gateway binding. Chunks are `chat.completion.chunk` with `choices[0].delta.{content \| reasoning \| reasoning_content \| tool_calls[{index, id?, function:{name?, arguments fragment}}]}`. Per-chunk `usage` is partial. The last data line is `{"response":"","usage":{…,"neurons"}}`, then `[DONE]`. Chunks carry a `p` padding field. | `stream.ts` forwards only `delta.content` and takes usage from the final summary line. Narration doesn't use tools, so tool-call fragments aren't reassembled. |
| AI Gateway | Only the `default` ID is created automatically. Any other ID fails with `AiGatewayError` "2001: Please configure AI Gateway in the Cloudflare dashboard" until it exists. **`incident-copilot` was created in the dashboard** (2026-10-15). | This is a configuration error, not an outage: both models fail the same way, so it maps to `LlmUnavailable` (rule path) and is logged as an error. Terraform (WP-25) imports the gateway if the provider supports it (R1). |
| glm tool grammar (*WP-07 live*) | glm builds a constrained grammar from each tool's JSON schema and fails with `3030: Grammar error: … look-around … is not supported` on the `dns_lookup` hostname regex. With the regex in the schema, **every planning call that fell back to glm would fail.** | `toolParameters()` drops `pattern` from LLM tool schemas; the hostname gets a `description` instead. |
| Last turn (*WP-07 live*) | Told "This is your last turn: call submit_findings now", glm still asked for `dns_lookup` + `http_probe`. With only `submit_findings` offered, it returned valid findings (IDs, not sentences) in about 6 s. | WP-09 passes `llmTools({finishOnly: true})` on the last turn. |
| Streamed narration (*WP-07 live*) | gpt streamed about 180 content deltas (reasoning deltas arrive separately and are dropped). It cites with **full-width brackets `【http-1】`**, which the citation check would have stripped. Once, a stream came back as a single run of `!!!…`; it didn't happen again in later runs. A narration cost about 40–55 neurons. | `citations.ts` turns `【id】` into `[id]` first. `isWellFormedNarration()` (needs the "Likely cause" label and a known `[id]`) lets WP-08 swap junk for `renderTemplate`. The prompt asks for plain square brackets. |
| Errors | Thrown as `InferenceUpstreamError` with `name` `AiError` or `AiGatewayError` and a numeric code at the start of `message` (`5007: No such model …`, `2001: …`). 429 and "allocation exceeded" weren't observed. | `client.ts` parses the code from the message. Rate-limit and allocation errors are matched by message as planned. Fixture: `test/fixtures/llm/errors.json`. |

import type { Fetch } from "../lib/doh";

/** Run bounds (TDD §5.2). */
export const MAX_TOOL_CALLS = 6;
export const MAX_PLAN_TURNS = 3;
export const RUN_DEADLINE_MS = 60_000;
export const SUBREQUEST_BUDGET = 45;
export const HTTP_SAMPLES = 3;
export const SNIPPET_TO_LLM = 500;
export const EVIDENCE_DIGEST_MAX = 1536;
export const HIGH_LATENCY_TOTAL_MS = 2000;
export const HIGH_LATENCY_TTFB_MS = 1500;
/** Per LLM call. gpt once took 19 s on a forced submit_findings turn; a timeout moves to the fast fallback. */
export const LLM_TIMEOUT_MS = 25_000;
/** Workers AI free allocation per day, and where the budget guard switches to rule-based mode (WP-17). */
export const NEURON_DAILY = 10_000;
export const BUDGET_GUARD_AT = 9_000;

export class BudgetExceededError extends Error {
  override name = "BudgetExceededError";
}

/**
 * Per-run subrequest budget. Serialisable so it can live in the run row between Workflow steps.
 * A tool reserves its worst case up front, then settles with what it actually used.
 */
export class SubrequestBudget {
  constructor(
    readonly limit: number = SUBREQUEST_BUDGET,
    private used = 0,
    private reserved = 0,
  ) {}

  static from(json: { limit: number; used: number; reserved?: number }): SubrequestBudget {
    return new SubrequestBudget(json.limit, json.used, json.reserved ?? 0);
  }

  toJSON() {
    return { limit: this.limit, used: this.used, reserved: this.reserved };
  }

import { introspectWorkflow } from "cloudflare:test";
import { env } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { incidentAgent } from "../../worker/agent/stub";
import { newId } from "../../worker/lib/ids";

/**
 * Real DiagnosisWorkflow runs: local Workflows, the mock LLM (LLM_MODE=mock) and the fake internet from
 * vitest.config.ts (broken.example.com → 500, ok.example.com → 200, DoH for *.example.com).
 */
async function diagnoseRun(message: string, target: string) {
  const introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW);
  try {
    const id = newId();
    const agent = await incidentAgent(env, id);
    const { runId } = await agent.init({ workspaceId: newId(), target, origin: "chat", message });
    expect(await agent.launchRun(runId!)).toBe(`diag-${id}-1`);
    const [instance] = await introspector.get();
    await instance!.waitForStatus("complete");
    return { id, agent, runId: runId!, output: await instance!.getOutput(), snapshot: await agent.getSnapshot() };
  } finally {
    await introspector.dispose();
  }
}

const finished = (s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof diagnoseRun>>["snapshot"]) => s.timeline.filter((t) => t.type === "run_finished").map((t) => t.detail.status);

describe("DiagnosisWorkflow (mock LLM, fake internet)", () => {
  it("diagnoses a failing origin end to end", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output, runId } = await diagnoseRun("why is broken.example.com down?", "https://broken.example.com/");
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "ai", toolCalls: 2, llmTurns: 2, category: "origin_5xx" });
    expect(s.checks.map((c) => [c.evidenceId, c.status])).toEqual([
      ["dns-1", "pass"],
      ["http-1", "fail"],
    ]);
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", status: "investigating", activeRunId: null, mode: "ai" });
    const narration = s.messages.find((m) => m.id === `narr-${runId}`)!;
    expect(narration.content).toContain("[http-1]");
    expect(finished(s)).toEqual(["done"]);
  });

  it("resolves a healthy site on its first run", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s } = await diagnoseRun("is ok.example.com up?", "https://ok.example.com/");
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "healthy", status: "resolved", activeRunId: null });
  });

  it("falls back to the rules when the LLM is down", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output } = await diagnoseRun("broken.example.com #llm-down", "https://broken.example.com/");
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "llm_unavailable", mode: "rule_based", llmTurns: 1 });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", mode: "rule_based", activeRunId: null });
    expect(s.checks.map((c) => c.tool)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe"]);
    expect(s.messages.at(-1)!.content).toContain("- HTTP probe: HTTP 500/500/500");
    expect(finished(s)).toEqual(["llm_unavailable"]);
  });

  it("goes to the rules after two invalid calls", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output } = await diagnoseRun("broken.example.com #llm-invalid", "https://broken.example.com/");

    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", activeRunId: null });
